# === 主窗口 / 关于 ===
Default Menu Key: Insert=默认菜单按键：Insert
Transparency=透明
Makes the UI transparent when not focused.=让REF在未选中时变得透明。
Input Passthrough=UI透过
Allows mouse and keyboard inputs to register to the game while the UI is focused.=让鼠标能够穿过REF界面点击到游戏内。
REFramework is currently initializing...=REFramework当前正在初始化……
This menu will close after initialization if you have the remember option enabled.=如果启用了"保存"选项，此菜单将在初始化后关闭。

# --- 无限消耗品 Infinite Consumables (1020) 补充 ---
[Infinite Consumables] Options##Infinite_Consumables=[无限消耗品] 选项##Infinite_Consumables
'Infinite' consumables will not be removed from your Pouch on use.="无限"消耗品使用时不会从背包中移除。
If not 'Infinite', used 'Refundable' consumables will be handed back to you when entering a Tent,=如果不是"无限"模式，使用的"可退还"消耗品会在进入帐篷时退还，
Any overflow will go to your Box, e.g. if you picked up or crafted more of a consumable.=溢出的道具会放入道具箱，例如你捡到或制作了更多的消耗品。
Other items you have picked up may also push Refunds to the Box if your Pouch is full,=你捡到的其他道具也可能将退还的消耗品推入道具箱（如果背包已满），
You are in 'Multiplayer' when on a Quest in a Lobby, joining an SoS, or after launching=当你在大厅中执行任务、加入SoS、或发射
an SoS flare which can find Players from an Online Single Player hunt.=SoS信号弹（可从在线单人狩猎中找到玩家）时，视为"多人游戏"模式。
returning to Base from a non-Survey Quest, or Save & Quitting.=从非调查任务返回据点，或保存并退出时生效。
so don't rely on this alone to keep your Loadout intact.=所以不要仅依赖此功能来保持装备配置完整。

# --- Auto Element 补充翻译 (1099) Tooltips & Texts ---
Enabled   =启用   
Enabled    Change Keybind=启用    更改按键绑定
Listening... =正在监听... 
Not Set=未设置
Auto Element Enabled=已启用自动属性变换
Auto Element Disabled=已禁用自动属性变换
  Supports both keyboard and controller.  =  同时支持键盘和手柄。  
  Change the weapon's element based on the specific monster part hit, rather than the monster as a whole.  =  根据击中的怪物特定部位来切换武器属性，而不是基于整个怪物。  
  Having this enabled will change the weapon's base element which means it will show in the equipment and particles.\n  Disabling this will instead change the attack info on hit, which means the new element won't be visible, but will still affect damage.  =  启用此项将改变武器的基础属性，这意味着属性将显示在外观和特效中。\n  禁用此项则仅在击中时添加属性修正，此时武器不显属性特效，但仍会计算属性伤害。  
  Only change the element of weapons that already have an element.  =  仅对原版自带属性的武器进行属性变换。  
  If the weapon currently has a status effect (e.g. poison), do not replace it with an element.  =  如果武器带有异常状态（如毒、麻痹），则不将其替换为属性。  
Change the weapon's element based on the specific monster part hit, rather than the monster as a whole.=根据击中的怪物特定部位来切换武器属性，而不是基于整个怪物。
Having this enabled will change the weapon's base element which means it will show in the equipment and particles.\n  Disabling this will instead change the attack info on hit, which means the new element won't be visible, but will still affect damage.=启用此项将改变武器的基础属性，这意味着属性将显示在外观和特效中。\n  禁用此项则仅在击中时添加属性修正，此时武器不显属性特效，但仍会计算属性伤害。
Only change the element of weapons that already have an element.=仅对原版自带属性的武器进行属性变换。
If the weapon currently has a status effect (e.g. poison), do not replace it with an element.=如果武器带有异常状态（如毒、麻痹），则不将其替换为属性。

# --- 1099 Auto Element ---
Auto Element=自动属性切换
Enabled   =启用   
Change Keybind=更改快捷键
  Supports both keyboard and controller.  =  支持通过键盘或手柄控制。  
Change element per part=依据具体击中部位弱点切换
  Change the weapon's element based on the specific monster part hit, rather than the monster as a whole.  =  基于击中的怪物特定部位弱点切换武器属性，而不是针对怪物全身弱点估算。  
Change weapon base element=切换武器面板基础属性
  Having this enabled will change the weapon's base element which means it will show in the equipment and particles.\n  Disabling this will instead change the attack info on hit, which means the new element won't be visible, but will still affect damage.  =  启用此项将改变武器的基础面板属性，在装备及特效中均可见。\n  禁用此项将仅在短时间内临时改变系统攻击判定，不可见特效但仍对伤害有效。  
Only change if weapon has element=仅当武器自带属性时切换
  Only change the element of weapons that already have an element.  =  只为原本就附带属性的武器触发自动属性切换。  
Don't replace status effects=不覆盖异常状态
  If the weapon currently has a status effect (e.g. poison), do not replace it with an element.  =  如果当前附加的是异常状态（比如中毒等），则不要将其替换为任何属性。  
Auto Element Enabled=自动属性开启
Auto Element Disabled=自动属性关闭
Listening... =监听按键中... 
Not Set=未设定

# --- 提示类文本 ---
Tips=提示
Doesn't affect chain settings values=不影响链设置值
Doesn't affect nipple color values and doesn't affect wether areola blend can be set=不影响乳头颜色值，也不影响是否可以设置乳晕混合
Nipple color saves with the FNM body preset.=乳头颜色随FNM身体预设保存。
The group numbers used for the chain groups can be changed and saved to a preset here to accommodate the chain file.=链组使用的组编号可以在此更改并保存到预设中，以适配链文件。
group number is an int for the order it is in the chain file, ex. 0 for the first group.=组编号是链文件中的顺序整数，例如0代表第一组。
| Category Filter:=| 分类筛选:

# --- 热键提示 ---
L3 + LB is the hotkey for gamepad. Numpad 0 is the hotkey for keyboard.=手柄热键: L3 + LB，键盘热键: 小键盘0
LB + A is the hotkey for gamepad. Numpad 2 is the hotkey for keyboard.=手柄热键: LB + A，键盘热键: 小键盘2
LB + L3 is the hotkey for gamepad. Numpad 4 is the hotkey for keyboard.=手柄热键: LB + L3，键盘热键: 小键盘4
LB + LT is the hotkey for gamepad. Numpad 7 is the hotkey for keyboard.=手柄热键: LB + LT，键盘热键: 小键盘7
LB + R3 is the hotkey for gamepad. Numpad 6 is the hotkey for keyboard.=手柄热键: LB + R3，键盘热键: 小键盘6
LB + RB is the hotkey for gamepad. Numpad 8 is the hotkey for keyboard.=手柄热键: LB + RB，键盘热键: 小键盘8
LB + Y is the hotkey for gamepad. Numpad 5 is the hotkey for keyboard.=手柄热键: LB + Y，键盘热键: 小键盘5
LT + LB is the hotkey for gamepad. Numpad 9 is the hotkey for keyboard.=手柄热键: LT + LB，键盘热键: 小键盘9
No hotkeys for Kanya, button required to cycle outfit.=Kanya无热键，需点击按钮切换套装。
No hotkeys for Luciella, button required to cycle outfit.=Luciella无热键，需点击按钮切换套装。
No hotkeys for Rochelle, button required to cycle outfit.=Rochelle无热键，需点击按钮切换套装。
No keybinds for Alessa EO, the UI button for cycle will have to be clicked.=Alessa EO无快捷键，需点击UI按钮切换。
No keybinds for Mina EO, the UI button for cycle will have to be clicked.=Mina EO无快捷键，需点击UI按钮切换。
No keybinds for Nadia EO, the UI button for cycle will have to be clicked.=Nadia EO无快捷键，需点击UI按钮切换。
Keep in mind, you may need to 'scan for actors' again in certain siutations.=请注意，在某些情况下你可能需要再次"扫描角色"。
Vanilla parts for autoswitch go by their same name for what is 'currently equipped'=自动切换的原版部件以当前装备的名称为准
Specific armors take precendence over autoswitches for all armors.=特定护甲的设置优先于所有护甲的自动切换。
To use ELA for autoswitch, specify the armor name by slot number name.=要使用ELA自动切换，请按槽位编号名称指定护甲名。
Selected armors to autoswitch fbx skel for are only switched to if all parts specified are equipped.=选定的自动切换fbx骨架的护甲，只有在所有指定部件都已装备时才会切换。
The purpose of the 'make giant' button is to tell who/where an actor is. Does not affect 'current scale'.="变巨大"按钮用于确认角色的位置，不影响"当前缩放"。
Scoutfly and/or player light removal depends on how it is set inside 'Waist Armor Visibility Settings'=导虫和/或玩家灯光移除取决于"腰部护甲可见性设置"中的配置
Last saved preset is the startup preset, or preset marked for autoswitch if the correct armor is equipped.\nOther settings that don't have a dedicated 'Save Preset' button are automatically saved.=最后保存的预设为启动预设，或在装备正确护甲时标记为自动切换的预设。\n没有专用"保存预设"按钮的其他设置会自动保存。

# --- 长文本/提示/描述 ---
group number is an int for the order it is in the chain file, ex. 1 for the first group.=组编号是链文件中的顺序整数，例如1代表第一组。
This the identifier for use with custom labels for body parts enabled and custom bones.=这是用于身体部件启用和自定义骨骼的自定义标签标识符。
This the identifier for use with custom labels for leg parts enabled and custom bones.=这是用于腿部部件启用和自定义骨骼的自定义标签标识符。
Note that this is different than the skin color parameter normally used by the game\nSo they can be mixed for different color variations.=注意这与游戏通常使用的肤色参数不同\n因此可以混合产生不同的颜色变化。
Note that this might help animations from being stuck.\nNegative values can also be used.=注意这可能有助于防止动画卡住。\n也可以使用负值。
This setting can be good to play with for when not using upscaling\nand when using AA without upscaling.=当不使用超采样且使用无超采样的AA时，\n调整此设置可能效果不错。
This is the delay in seconds for the sliders to kick in\nwhen equipment refreshes.\nThis is done to prevent physics issues\nwhen physics bone scales are uneven.\n(Default = 0.01 cpu seconds)\nWithout physics bones with unevenly scaled slider\nvalues for them, you can get away with\nsetting this to zero.=这是装备刷新时滑块生效的延迟秒数。\n用于防止物理骨骼缩放不均匀时的物理问题。\n（默认 = 0.01 CPU秒）\n如果没有缩放不均匀的物理骨骼滑块值，\n可以将此值设为零。
Some things dynamically enable/disable normally in the game.\nOverriding parts enabled will set them how you have it.=游戏中某些内容会正常动态启用/禁用。\n覆盖部件启用将按你的设置固定。
Things like goggles and the smithy glove are used as separate objects\nthat dynamically enable/disable normally in the game.\nOverriding parts enabled will set them how you have it.=护目镜和铁匠手套等作为独立对象\n在游戏中正常动态启用/禁用。\n覆盖部件启用将按你的设置固定。
Things like the book are used as separate objects\nthat dynamically enable/disable normally in the game.\nOverriding parts enabled will set them how you have it.=书等作为独立对象在游戏中正常动态启用/禁用。\n覆盖部件启用将按你的设置固定。
Some of them like Custom Bone 1 & 2 are linked together in the sliders. They are intended to be\nset up for a pair of breasts for example.\nThe sliders will modify both CustomBone 1 & 2 at the same time for symmetry purposes.=其中如自定义骨骼1和2在滑块中是关联的。\n它们设计用于例如一对胸部。\n滑块会同时修改自定义骨骼1和2以保持对称。
Some of them like Custom Bone 9 & 10 are linked together in the sliders. They are intended to be\nset up for L and R butt cheeks for example.\nThe sliders will modify both CustomBone 9 & 10 at the same time for symmetry purposes.=其中如自定义骨骼9和10在滑块中是关联的。\n它们设计用于例如左右臀瓣。\n滑块会同时修改自定义骨骼9和10以保持对称。
You will need to click the refresh the button\nif you change character skin tone\ncolor mid-game and want it to set to that color.=如果在游戏中更改了角色肤色\n并希望设置为该颜色，\n你需要点击刷新按钮。
Rey Dau is set to work a bit differently.\nInstead, the waist knife will be de-\nconstructed for both waist light\nand waist knife removal.=雷电龙的设置有所不同。\n腰刀将被拆解，\n同时移除腰灯和腰刀。
\nIMPORTANT=\n重要提示
Modifying the physics values works differently than the bones.\nSo, when switching physics to no longer use the custom chain settings,\nthe chain will need to be refreshed for the original values to be used.=修改物理值与修改骨骼的工作方式不同。\n因此，当切换回不使用自定义链设置时，\n需要刷新链才能使用原始值。
If the game sets its own parts enabled, it will override these settings.\nYou may want to try a different method of hiding/showing the part.=如果游戏设置了自己的部件启用，它将覆盖这些设置。\n你可能需要尝试其他方法来隐藏/显示该部件。
Note that this does not affect\nautoswitch if there is no ELA\nassociated with the selected\narmor to affect.=注意如果没有与选定\n护甲关联的ELA，\n则不影响自动切换。
Note that this does not apply to multiple occurances of buff glow effects such as using demon powder\nand other items at the same time.=注意这不适用于同时使用鬼人粉尘\n等多个增益发光效果的情况。
Enabling or disabling autoswitch, as well as setting autoswitch behavior will need to have the preset saved to take effect.=启用或禁用自动切换，以及设置自动切换行为，需要保存预设才能生效。
After changing the autoswitch value and saving it,\nscripts will need to be reset and any armor change to take effect.=更改自动切换值并保存后，\n需要重置脚本并更换护甲才能生效。
May need to requip equipment for the original chain file settings to be used if you disable this.=如果禁用此功能，可能需要重新装备才能使用原始链文件设置。
Setting the chain group in the advanced settings and selecting the body/leg\nparts for the sliders to affect will be saved only when using the=在高级设置中设置链组并选择滑块影响的身体/腿部\n部件，仅在使用
You can still add chain, clsp, or jcns, to\nan armor that wouldn=你仍然可以为不需要的护甲\n添加链、clsp或jcns
LT + A (Keyboard U) Animation=LT + A（键盘U）动画
LT + B (Keyboard T) Animation=LT + B（键盘T）动画
L_FNMNipple and R_FNMNipple for the nipples.=L_FNMNipple和R_FNMNipple用于乳头。
Hotkey for gamepad is LT only while talking to Carla (Quests menu).=手柄热键仅在与Carla对话时（任务菜单）为LT。
Hotkey for gamepad is LT only while talking to Felicita (Manage squads menu).=手柄热键仅在与Felicita对话时（管理小队菜单）为LT。
Hotkey for gamepad is LT only while talking to GrandHubBartendress (Quests menu).=手柄热键仅在与集会所女调酒师对话时（任务菜单）为LT。

# --- 含撇号的UI标签/护甲名 ---
'Player To Affect' is by player name. Type in any player name for the character you want to apply the mod to.="影响的玩家"按玩家名称指定。输入要应用mod的角色的玩家名。
Diver's Belt A=潜水员腰带A
Diver's Belt B=潜水员腰带B
Can set Custom 'Alt Arkveld A' lightning effect?=可以设置自定义"替代冥渊龙A"闪电效果？
Doesn't affect chain setting values=不影响链设置值
Doesn't affect nipple color values=不影响乳头颜色值
Doesn't affect nipple color values or if areola blend can be set=不影响乳头颜色值或乳晕混合度是否可设置
The FNM-named custom bones aren't necessarily needed for the chain groups (jiggle settings to work).=FNM命名的自定义骨骼不一定需要用于链组（晃动设置）。
These bones can be setup in armatures for different items, (ex. Hope Greaves with Custom Bone 5 & 6).=这些骨骼可以在不同装备的骨架中设置（例如希望护腿配合自定义骨骼5和6）。
These bones can be setup in armatures for different items, (ex. Hope Greaves with Custom Bone 9 & 10.=这些骨骼可以在不同装备的骨架中设置（例如希望护腿配合自定义骨骼9和10）。
If you would like to add a sound, you can do so by specifying a path to a .wav file, relative to the game's exe.=如果要添加音效，可以指定相对于游戏exe的.wav文件路径。
If your modded outfit is specified in JSON as 'Default Outfit' for 'OutfitName' then it may not show up here. Not likely, but worth noting.=如果你的mod套装在JSON中的'OutfitName'指定为'Default Outfit'，则可能不会显示在这里。不太可能，但值得注意。
Enabling or disabling autoswitch, as well as setting autoswitch behavior will need to have the preset saved in order to apply.=启用或禁用自动切换以及设置自动切换行为，需要保存预设才能生效。
Hotkey for gamepad is RT only while talking to Alma. Hotkey for keyboard is Numpad 1 at any time Alma is around.=手柄热键仅在与Alma对话时为RT。键盘热键为小键盘1（Alma在附近时随时可用）。
Hotkey for gamepad is RT only while talking to Gemma. Hotkey for keyboard is Numpad 3 at any time Gemma is around.=手柄热键仅在与Gemma对话时为RT。键盘热键为小键盘3（Gemma在附近时随时可用）。

Modifying the physics values works differently than the bones.\nSo, when switching physics to no longer be affected,\nkeep in mind that means the original physics settings won't be\nrestored unless you reload the top/pants respectivly.=物理参数与骨骼参数的修改方式不同。停止覆盖物理参数后，原始物理设置不会立即恢复，需要重新加载对应的上装或裤装。
If the game sets its own parts enabled, it will override these settings. You may want to try a different slot if that is the case.\nThere is also an option to prevent the game from setting parts enable/disable for certain parts.=游戏自身设置部件显隐时会覆盖这里的设置。遇到此问题可尝试其他部位，或启用阻止游戏修改指定部件显隐的选项。
Parts Enabled that are set to invisible/unchecked and marked with\n'(False for EO Alt Chain Usage)' use specified alternate chain files for extra outfits.\nThere are secondary and tertiary alternate chains to swap.\nThis useful for a combination such as\nChain1 = Bra + Pants, Chain2 = No Bra + Pants, Chain3 = Bra + No Pants, Chain4 = No Bra + No Pants;\nto compensate for more realistic variances in jiggle.\nMore info in the Extra Outfits section on that.	标有“False for EO Alt Chain Usage”的部件复选框取消勾选后，会为额外服装切换指定的备用 Chain（物理链）文件。还支持第二、第三套备用物理链，例如分别用于内衣与裤装都保留、仅移除内衣、仅移除裤装、两者都移除，以匹配不同状态的摆动效果。详见额外服装说明。
'Enable Mesh Parts on Preset Switch?' is meant to change the way parts enable is handled upon changing the preset.\nThe default behavior is to respect override parts enabled on preset switches.\nHowever, sometimes you might want parts to be re-enabled first if you are switching\nto a different preset with override parts enabled unchecked.\nThe behavior you have set will be preset specific and affect both manual switching and autoswitching of presets.\nThe behavior when checked enables all mesh parts and then applies your PE preset settings.\nKeep in mind, the setting to enable all first has the potential drawback to\nset parts enabled that would otherwise be disabled by the game, hence why 'Override Parts Enabled?' is even an option in the first place.=“切换预设时启用网格部件”决定切换预设时的显隐处理方式。默认遵循预设的显隐覆盖设置；勾选后先启用全部网格部件，再应用预设的部件设置。此项按预设保存，手动切换与自动切换均适用。先启用全部部件可能使游戏原本隐藏的部件也显示出来，因此请按需使用。
'Alt Chain Path' or alternate chains are meant to be used in conjunction with specific parts enable settings.\nAs an example, let's say you have an outfit with hard armor plate around the breast area.\nThen, with parts enable you want to remove that armor plate and now it's bare tits.\n Well, obviously there would be a difference in breast jiggle between the two.\nYou can use two different chain files with extra outfits.\nOne will be used with a certain combonation of parts enable settings.\nThe alternate will be used if any of the desginated 'False for EO Alt Chain Usage' (EO meaning extra outfits)\nparts enabled checkboxes are set unchecked/false.=Alt Chain Path（备用物理链路径）与部件显隐设置配合使用。例如，移除胸部的硬质护甲后，摆动效果应当随之变化。可为额外服装指定不同的 Chain 文件；取消勾选标有“False for EO Alt Chain Usage”的指定部件后，将使用备用文件。
JSON parsing structure: 'OutfitName' (your outfit's name to show up in the info as). Paths: 'OutfitMeshPath', 'OutfitMaterialPath', 'OutfitChainPath', 'OutfitExtraPEChainPath', 'OutfitExtraPEChainPath2', 'OutfitExtraPEChainPath3'.\nCan leave out paths or set them to 'nil' to not use.\nPaths are relative to natives\\STM.=JSON 结构：OutfitName 为服装显示名称；路径字段为 OutfitMeshPath、OutfitMaterialPath、OutfitChainPath、OutfitExtraPEChainPath、OutfitExtraPEChainPath2、OutfitExtraPEChainPath3。不使用的路径可省略或设为字符串 'nil'。所有路径相对于 natives\\STM。
'OutfitJcnsPath' (This has a special parsing structure.\nYou can use as many Jcns files as you want, just be sure to include a suffix for each individual entry.\n(example: 'OutfitJcnsPath1', 'OutfitJcnsPath2')\nThe value for these entries also have a special parsing structure.\nAn example being 'MyOutfitMod\\JointConstraintsFile.jcns(true)((2))(((1.0)))'\nThis can be broken down into path (string), enable update timing override (boolean),\nupdate timing mode (int between 0-3), blend rate (System.Single).\nMore info for what these are set to is displayed within the EO information.=OutfitJcnsPath 使用特殊结构，可通过 OutfitJcnsPath1、OutfitJcnsPath2 等后缀指定多个文件。示例：MyOutfitMod\\JointConstraintsFile.jcns(true)((2))(((1.0)))。各部分依次表示文件路径、是否覆盖更新时间、更新模式（0～3）以及混合比例（System.Single）。详见额外服装信息。
This is the delay in seconds for the sliders to kick in\nwhen equipment refreshes.\nThis is done to prevent physics issues\nwhen physics bone scales are uneven.\n(Default = 0.01 cpu seconds)\nWithout physics bones with unevenly scaled slider\nvalues for them, you can get away with\nsetting this to zero.	装备刷新后，滑块将延迟指定秒数再生效，以减少物理骨骼缩放不一致造成的问题。默认延迟为 0.01 个 CPU 计时秒。如果未对物理骨骼设置不一致的缩放值，可尝试设为 0。
If the game sets its own parts enabled, it will override these settings. You may want to try a different slot if that is the case.\nThere is also an option to prevent the game from setting parts enable/disable for certain parts.\nA useful example is when the Alma's vanilla default outfit backpack\nstraps won't disable. The option can be used to overrule the game setting it.=游戏自身设置部件显隐时会覆盖这里的设置。遇到问题可尝试其他部位，或阻止游戏修改指定部件。比如阿尔玛原版默认服装的背包带无法隐藏时，可使用该选项覆盖游戏设置。
If you are wondering why Default + Scrivener's Coat shows the default jacket when setting some parts enabled to true,\nit's because the game would otherwise have those parts disabled for the design of the outfit.=“默认服装＋书记官外套”在启用某些部件后显示默认夹克，是因为游戏原本会为该套服装隐藏这些部件。

Material presets are saved to: 'reframework\\data\\FBSPresets\\Materials\\{PartType}\\'.\nCustom textures can be loaded from: 'reframework\\data\\FBSStartupData\\Textures\\'.\nThe parsing structure for json that goes there is just list of string paths to your custom texture(s).\nPaths are relative to natives/stm.\nBe sure to use enclosing '[]' rather than parenthesis for this.\nPreset notes are useful for anything you might want to include such as something like a description,\nor a specific file asset version this is meant to be used with.=材质预设保存在 reframework\\data\\FBSPresets\\Materials\\{PartType}\\。自定义纹理可从 reframework\\data\\FBSStartupData\\Textures\\ 加载。JSON 内容应为自定义纹理路径的字符串列表，路径相对于 natives/stm，并使用 [] 包围。预设备注可填写说明、适用资源文件版本等信息。

Physics presets can be found in Reframework/data/FBSPresets/Physics/{PartType}/\nAvoid changing scenes or equipment while actively editing colliders.\nDoing so may cause the editor UI to become desynchronized from the current collider values.\nReopen/restart the editor if the displayed values no longer match the colliders.\nPhysics types supported for editing: Chain2, Clsp, and Gpuc.\nArmors that do not make use of these will not show editors for whichever one(s) are not in use.\nSymmetry is applied when a value changes after the setting has been enabled.\nExisting values (that are potentially asymmetrical) are left unchanged.\nThe notes section is for anything you might want to add as a note in your preset.\nFor example, 'this preset is inteded to be used with this specific clsp file asset' or something like that.\nKeep in mind that same edited values can still produce a different visual result depending on how the file asset is set up.\nFor example an offset value can change the look depending on the starting value.\nAn absolute value adjustment can change depending on the order of its attributes\nor if previous absolute value saved data is applied.=物理预设保存在 Reframework/data/FBSPresets/Physics/{PartType}/。编辑碰撞体时请避免切换场景或装备，否则界面数值可能与实际状态不同步；发生后请重新打开编辑器。支持 Chain2、Clsp、Gpuc，防具未使用的类型不会显示对应编辑器。启用对称调整后，只在修改数值时同步，已有的不对称数值保持不变。备注可说明适用的资源文件。相同修改值在不同资源上可能呈现不同效果，偏移取决于初始值，绝对值还可能受属性顺序及此前已应用数据影响。
For each custom bone you want to use in the armature, they need to be named CustomBone1,\nCustomBone2, CustomBone3, etc.. to be setup for slider modification.=需要通过滑块调整的自定义骨骼，必须依次命名为 CustomBone1、CustomBone2、CustomBone3 等。

If the game sets its own parts enabled, it will override these settings.\nYou may want to try a different slot if that is the case.=游戏自身设置部件显隐时会覆盖这里的设置。遇到此问题可尝试其他部位。
Parts Enabled that are set to invisible/unchecked and marked with\n'(False for ELA Alt Chain and CLSP Usage)' use specified alternate chain or clsp files for extra\nlayered armor. This is useful for chain if, for example, you had hard metal plate\ncovering boobs and then wanted to remove the plate. Obviously there\nwould be a difference in jiggle hence why disabling\na part specified can automatically swap chain files.\nThis is useful for clsp to provide different collision sizes based on parts being enabled/disabled.\nIf you do not specify an alternate chain/clsp, they will not be used and the originals will be.\nThey also work independently, so you don't need both chain and clsp for it to work.=标有“False for ELA Alt Chain and CLSP Usage”的部件取消勾选后，将为额外外观防具切换指定的备用 Chain 或 Clsp 文件。例如移除胸部硬甲后，可切换摆动或碰撞参数。未指定备用文件时继续使用原文件。物理链和碰撞文件彼此独立，无需同时提供。
Mesh parts that are hidden by default are not affected by the 'Can Set Mesh Parts' setting.\nMesh parts hidden by default are intended\nto be used with extra parts that would otherwise clip\nor not work well with the 'main' default outfit/armor look.=默认隐藏的网格部件不受“允许设置网格部件”影响。这些部件通常用于额外配件，默认显示可能与主服装穿模或不协调。

Parts Enabled that are set to invisible/unchecked and marked with\n'(False for ELA Alt Chain and CLSP Usage)' use specified alternate chain or clsp files for extra layered armor.\nThis is useful for chain if, for example, you had pants covering your character's butt\nand then wanted to remove them.\nObviously there would be a difference in jiggle hence why disabling\na part specified can automatically swap chain files.\nThis is useful for clsp to provide different collision sizes based on parts being enabled/disabled.\nIf you do not specify an alternate chain/clsp, they will not be used and the originals will be.\nThey also work independently, so you don't need both chain and clsp for it to work.=标有“False for ELA Alt Chain and CLSP Usage”的部件取消勾选后，将为额外外观防具切换指定的备用 Chain 或 Clsp 文件。例如移除胸部硬甲后，可切换摆动或碰撞参数。未指定备用文件时继续使用原文件。物理链和碰撞文件彼此独立，无需同时提供。
Before modifying the FNM custom bones, I encourage you to check out modifying the regular,\nother bones first, as the FNM bones are parented to them.=调整 FNM 自定义骨骼前，建议先尝试常规骨骼，因为 FNM 骨骼是它们的子级。
Setting the chain group in the advanced settings and selecting the body/leg\nparts for the sliders to affect will be saved only when using the 'Save Preset' button.\nChanging them initially does not save to the preset but does allow you to use\nthe settings you have entered=高级设置中的物理链组和滑块作用的身体／腿部部件，仅在点击“保存预设”后写入预设。修改后可以立即使用，但不会自动保存。
\nThese sliders are setup and meant to be used with Female Nude Mod, however if the bones/and or chains are setup for it, these sliders can be used for them.=这些滑块针对 Female Nude Mod（女性身体模组）设计；其他资源的骨骼或物理链采用兼容结构时也可使用。

Use Direct Memory Writes For Sliders?=滑块使用直接内存写入？
Using direct memory writes will lead to faster performance. However, game updates can break this, in which case, disable the feature so that it will work.=直接写入内存可提高性能，但游戏更新可能使其失效。遇到此情况请关闭此项，以恢复正常工作。

ELA Switch Frame Delay Multiplier for VFX=ELA 切换时特效重新触发的帧延迟倍率
This is used as a multiplier for frame delay to retrigger effects.\nToo early will cause the effect to not show back up.\nToo late will be visually jarring.\nThis is dependent on framerate.=设置重新触发特效的帧延迟倍率。过早会导致特效无法重新出现，过晚会造成明显的视觉跳变；效果与帧率有关。
You can still add chain, clsp, or jcns, to\nan armor that wouldn't otherwise normally be call for them.\nIn other words as an example, you do not need pfbs to add chain\nphysics to an armor that wouldn't otherwise have it/call for it.=即使防具原本不使用 Chain、Clsp 或 Jcns，也可以为其添加这些资源。例如，无需修改 Pfb 即可为原本没有物理链的防具添加物理效果。
The sequence builder is meant to build animation 'sequences' that can be saved and played\nfrom the animation player.\nFor the time being, if you need to change actor name or motion list (or anything else not editable from the sequence object)\nfor a set, you'll need to do so from the json.=序列构建器用于创建、保存可在动画播放器中播放的动画序列。目前，角色名称、动作列表及序列对象中无法编辑的其他内容，需要在 JSON 文件中修改。
Interpolation frames, etc.. only work when blending animations with vanilla animations.\nIt also only works at the start of the animation, not the end.=插值帧等设置仅在自定义动画与原版动画混合时生效，并且只作用于动画开头，不作用于结尾。
For swap bodies, you can provide identifiers for them so that way they can work with various preset functions, just the same as\nselected x to affect already does. For example, if you have a swap body for Gemma, you can add an entry to the swap body json\n'GemmaSwapBodyName'. This name will be added to Gemma body sliders aviablable for specific presets and current outfit.\nNote that you must at least have a mesh specified/associated to be counted as an 'outfit' and load.\nFor other NPCs, simply replace 'Gemma' inside the key with their name.=可为替换身体指定标识，使其参与预设功能。例如，在杰玛替换身体的 JSON 中添加 GemmaSwapBodyName，该名称会出现在杰玛滑块的特定预设与当前服装选项中。必须至少关联一个网格，才能作为服装加载。其他角色请将字段中的 Gemma 替换为对应名称。
Do not give the same actors the same sequence order.\nFor example, if you have two player sequence objects, the sequence order would be '1' for the first and '2' for the second.\nIf you have a player and Gemma sequence object, then your sequence order for both of them would be 1 to play at the same time.=同一角色的多个序列对象不能使用相同顺序。例如，玩家的两个序列应分别设置为 1 和 2；若玩家与杰玛需要同时播放，两者都可设置为 1。
For 'List Current Character Scales', these are scales that are applied to the characters in-game.\nSo for the files you work with out of the game inside Blender,\nit's possible they will be of a different scale or the '1.0' scale.\nThis is useful information to list before you make your animation so that you can resize your character to match the scale\nand have thing better align in-game. Note that this prints the *current* scale\nso that means if you have the scale being modified by something else like one of the slider mods,\nit could be printing the values from that instead of the vanilla values.\nThese are not bone scales, but rather the transform scales applied above the armature level.=“列出当前角色缩放”显示游戏中角色实际使用的缩放，可能与 Blender 中资源的缩放或 1.0 不同。制作动画前可据此调整模型尺寸，以便在游戏中对齐。这里输出的是当前值，可能已被其他滑块模组修改，并不一定是原版值。这些数值属于骨架上层的变换缩放，而非单个骨骼缩放。
For best results when playing an animation, try to hold your character still (don't hit extra buttons)\nand perform the animation on flat ground and get the characters close to each other.\nIf an actor other than the player character is frozen when attempting the animation,\nit may be because they are out of view in their original position.\nSome advice for making the animation(s) is to try and avoid moving the root bone higher or lower.\nThis will not be 1:1 with the game. It is probably best to just leave the root bone alone in your animation\nand if you need to offset it forwards/backwards/left/right while making your animations, set back at the end after finishing your animation(s).=播放动画时请尽量保持角色静止，不操作其他按键，并在平地上让角色彼此靠近。非玩家角色若冻结，可能是其原始位置不在视野内。制作动画时尽量不要上下移动根骨骼，游戏中不一定能完全对应；如制作时需要前后左右偏移，请在完成后复位。
Enable/disabling the IKLeg2 attribute can have different effects. The advantage is that the alignment between actors remains more stable. However, the disadvantage is that the feet may not adjust well to the ground.=切换 IKLeg2 属性会产生不同效果：启用后角色之间的对齐更稳定，但双脚可能无法很好地贴合地面。
Gemma\nAlma=杰玛\n阿尔玛
If you would like to add a line break for the description of your anim sequence,\nyou can do so by adding '\\n' to the json.\nSome actors support to play animations from their menus.\nThis is 'what text to show in GUI(s) for animation' is for.\nIt is independent of your keybind animations.\nIt will work automatically if an actor apart of the animation is found for the currently\nselected animation sequence.=如需在动画序列说明中换行，可在 JSON 中添加 \\n。部分角色支持从自身菜单播放动画，“动画在界面中显示的文字”用于这一功能，与快捷键动画相互独立。当前序列中的对应角色被找到后会自动生效。
If you don't want to specify a second part (i.e. autoswitch on this body but not legs,\nyou should just leave it blank (empty string)=如果不想指定第二个部位，例如仅在身体变化时自动切换、腿部不参与，请将对应字段留空。
Do not reset scripts while using custom fbx skel if you want to be able\nto switch back to the original in that game session.=使用自定义 FBX 骨架时，如果还需要在本次游戏中切回原骨架，请勿重置脚本。
You can make an autoswitch preset that is for just original skeleton only\nand the rest for specific armors/skeletons.=可以创建一个仅使用原始骨架的自动切换预设，其他预设用于指定防具或骨架。
Don't worry about the original skeleton being set over and over again.\nOnce it's set original, it won't set again unless it's not original.=无需担心原始骨架被重复设置。恢复原骨架后，只有再次切换成其他骨架时才会重新设置。
FBXSkel files go in 'reframework/data/FBSStartupData/FBXSkels/\nThe parsing structure is as follows:\n'FBXSkelPath' - Path (relative to natives/stm) to your fbxskel file.=FBX 骨架文件配置放在 reframework/data/FBSStartupData/FBXSkels/。JSON 字段 FBXSkelPath 为骨架文件路径，相对于 natives/stm。

Note that this does not apply to multiple occurances of buff glow effects such as using demon powder and hardshell powder together.=此项不作用于同时存在的多种增益发光，例如同时使用鬼人粉尘与硬化粉尘。

JSON can be provided inside FBSStartupData\\CustomBoneNames\\Body\\ to give custom bones names/labels.\nExmample labels/parsing structure:\n'ArmorName': 'ch03_002_0012',\n'CustomBone1&2Label': 'Whatever Bone Name'=可在 FBSStartupData\\CustomBoneNames\\Body\\ 中提供 JSON，为自定义骨骼指定名称／标签。\n标签及解析结构示例：\n'ArmorName': 'ch03_002_0012',\n'CustomBone1&2Label': 'Whatever Bone Name'
Type in a slider value manually to go above and below the desinged threshold of the blendshape mesh edits.\nThere are two separate 'gates' for being able to set blendshapes (for various reasons)\n1. Is the information used by your bone sliders ('Selected Armor to affect', etc)\n2. Is the value of 'Can Set BlendShapes for this Armor?'\n--\n'Selected Armor to affect', etc because if you have two different armors with same blendshape names,\nand let's say for armor to affect you have a particular armor,\nthen blendshapes would affect both which we don't want.\nAnd the 'Can Set BlendShapes for this Armor?' part of the sentence is a separate guard if you don't want game values being overwritten.\nOr in simpler terms, the first is the saftey guard for both bone sliders and blendshapes,\nthe second is the saftey guard for just blendshapes.\nMake sure both are set to work for the preset if you are trying to set blendshapes and also use the NR Edge Exclusion feature.\nThe can set gates affect the NR Edge Exclusion feature.\nAll of this being separate from the jcns-blendshape relationship (more on that ahead).\nIf the blendshape names show up and the mesh is supposed to have them,\nthere is a good chance it is working correctly.\nBlendshapes should show here if the armor has them regardless of the 'saftey guards'.\nSome blendshapes may be controlled by joint constraints, and will not keep set.\nIf you are using custom named blendshape(s) and the loaded jcns does not map to\nany of these blendshapes, you should not have to worry about this.\nBlendshape data belongs to the bone slider preset data.\nNote that when an arm and body mesh share blendshapes, adjustments can affect the other for seamless editing.\nResetting values works the same way.\nThe 'NREdgeExclusion' setting must be enabled to fix shading for some Blendshape meshes.\nAlternatively, you can edit this value in the pfb file.\nTo add Blendshapes that can be set on parts enable status,\ncreate json inside reframework/data/FBSStartupData/PartsEnableBlendshapes/{PartType}/\nPart type examples include Body, Leg, Helm, etc..\nThe parsing structure inside the json files is as follows: 'ArmorName' - Works the same as the PE custom labels,\ncorrespond this to a vanilla armor by path name or ELA by its custom labels ID.\n'MeshPart{Numbers 70-75}Blendshape' - value parsing structure is: {Blendshape name}((Disable={Your Enable No.},Enable={Your Enable No.}))\nExample of the full line; 'MeshPart70Blendshape': 'Test((Disable=0.0,Enable=1.0))'	可以手动输入数值，超出形态键滑块原定的范围。\n形态键由两个条件共同控制：一是骨骼滑块所选的目标防具，二是“允许调整此防具的形态键”。前者同时保护骨骼与形态键，后者只控制形态键，避免覆盖游戏数值。使用形态键或 NR 边缘排除功能时，请确认两个条件均已启用。未关联 ELA 的原版防具仍按各自条件处理。\n防具包含形态键时，即使未允许修改，也会显示名称。若形态键受关节约束控制，手动设置可能无法保持；自定义形态键未被当前 Jcns 映射时，通常不受此问题影响。形态键数据属于骨骼滑块预设，手臂与身体共享的形态键可能联动调整和重置。\n部分形态键网格需要启用 NREdgeExclusion 修复着色，也可在 Pfb 文件中修改。该功能受上述允许条件控制。\n如需随部件显隐切换形态键，在 reframework/data/FBSStartupData/PartsEnableBlendshapes/{PartType}/ 创建 JSON。PartType 可为 Body、Leg、Helm 等；ArmorName 与部件标签规则一致，原版防具使用路径名称，ELA 使用自定义标签 ID。\n示例字段：'MeshPart70Blendshape': 'Test((Disable=0.0,Enable=1.0))'。MeshPart70～75 对应部件编号，括号内指定禁用和启用时的数值。
Parts Enabled are saved on a per-armor basis and values that are set will\nchange depending on what you have equipped and saved.\nYou do not need to save your body sliders preset to save these changes.\nSettings for the particular armor are saved upon clicking the checkboxes\nYour values set for parts enabled are not tied/saved to the body preset.\nThey data for them, however, be found at\nReframework\\data\\FBSPresets\\BodyPartsEnable\\PartsEnable.json\nThis is a master preset that will update dynamically with the changes you've made\nand what armor it belongs to.\nCustom labels for parts enable can be set on a per armor basis.\nThe location for the json file indicated the info to do so needs to be placed at\nreframework\\data\\FBSStartupData\\PartsEnableNames\\Body\\\n"ArmorName": "ch03_002_0002",\n"MaterialPartLabel0": "Upper Torso Armor"\nMeshPartLabel0 being an example for the mesh parts.=部件显隐按防具分别保存，会随当前装备及已保存设置切换。点击复选框即保存，无需保存身体或腿部滑块预设，且不与该预设绑定。数据记录在 Reframework\\data\\FBSPresets\\BodyPartsEnable\\PartsEnable.json，该主预设会自动更新。自定义标签的 JSON 放在 reframework\\data\\FBSStartupData\\PartsEnableNames\\Body\\，使用 ArmorName 指定防具，MaterialPartLabel0 指定材质部件名称；网格部件使用 MeshPartLabel0 等字段。

Works the same as the PE custom labels,\ncorrespond this to a vanilla armor by path name or ELA by its custom labels ID.\n'MeshPart{Numbers 70-75}Blendshape' - value parsing structure is: {Blendshape name}((Disable={Your Enable No.},Enable={Your Enable No.}))\nExample of the full line; 'MeshPart70Blendshape': 'Test((Disable=0.0,Enable=1.0))'	可以手动输入数值，超出形态键滑块原定的范围。\n形态键由两个条件共同控制：一是骨骼滑块所选的目标防具，二是“允许调整此防具的形态键”。前者同时保护骨骼与形态键，后者只控制形态键，避免覆盖游戏数值。使用形态键或 NR 边缘排除功能时，请确认两个条件均已启用。未关联 ELA 的原版防具仍按各自条件处理。\n防具包含形态键时，即使未允许修改，也会显示名称。若形态键受关节约束控制，手动设置可能无法保持；自定义形态键未被当前 Jcns 映射时，通常不受此问题影响。形态键数据属于骨骼滑块预设，手臂与身体共享的形态键可能联动调整和重置。\n部分形态键网格需要启用 NREdgeExclusion 修复着色，也可在 Pfb 文件中修改。该功能受上述允许条件控制。\n如需随部件显隐切换形态键，在 reframework/data/FBSStartupData/PartsEnableBlendshapes/{PartType}/ 创建 JSON。PartType 可为 Body、Leg、Helm 等；ArmorName 与部件标签规则一致，原版防具使用路径名称，ELA 使用自定义标签 ID。\n示例字段：'MeshPart70Blendshape': 'Test((Disable=0.0,Enable=1.0))'。MeshPart70～75 对应部件编号，括号内指定禁用和启用时的数值。
Parts Enabled are saved on a per-armor basis and values that are set will\nchange depending on what you have equipped and saved.\nYou do not need to save your leg sliders preset to save these changes.\nSettings for the particular armor are saved upon clicking the checkboxes\nYour values set for parts enabled are not tied/saved to the leg preset.\nThey data for them, however, be found at\nReframework\\data\\FBSPresets\\LegPartsEnable\\PartsEnable.json\nThis is a master preset that will update dynamically with the changes you've made\nand what armor it belongs to.\nCustom labels for parts enable can be set on a per armor basis.\nThe location for the json file indicated the info to do so needs to be placed at\nreframework\\data\\FBSStartupData\\PartsEnableNames\\Leg\\\n"ArmorName": "ch03_002_0004",\n"MaterialPartLabel0": "Panties"\nMeshPartLabel0 being an example for the mesh parts.=部件显隐按防具分别保存，会随当前装备及已保存设置切换。点击复选框即保存，无需保存身体或腿部滑块预设，且不与该预设绑定。数据记录在 Reframework\\data\\FBSPresets\\LegPartsEnable\\PartsEnable.json，该主预设会自动更新。自定义标签的 JSON 放在 reframework\\data\\FBSStartupData\\PartsEnableNames\\Leg\\，使用 ArmorName 指定防具，MaterialPartLabel0 指定材质部件名称；网格部件使用 MeshPartLabel0 等字段。

This editor is designed to create json in order to be appended\nto ELA json. It is more of a dev tool to create this than a\nperpetual editor for different helms/hairs in the game.\nThe 'Get Current Adjust List' button is put in a dropdown after getting the hair adjust lists\nto prevent accidental button activations and redo the list before saving or adjusting the player.\nThere are a lot of values to be adjusted for just a singular helm.\nMy recommendation would be to equip whatever helm best suits\nyour needs as a starting point for your modded helm.\nThen, click the button 'Get Current Adjust List' to load those values into the editor.\nOnce you are done with your edits and want to add your adjust list settings to your ELA mod,\nlocate the json file for whatever preset you made/want to use,\ncopy all the contents in that file.\nNext, inside your ELA json, create a key for 'HelmHairAdjustList' and paste in what you copied from the helm hair adjust preset.=此编辑器主要用于制作可嵌入 ELA JSON 的头盔头发调整配置，属于资源制作工具，并非在游戏中长期管理所有头盔的编辑器。\n建议先装备与目标最接近的原版头盔，再点击“读取当前调整列表”。每个头盔可能包含大量参数。读取后按钮会收进下拉菜单，避免误触覆盖尚未保存的修改。\n完成编辑后，打开所保存预设的 JSON，复制全部内容；在 ELA JSON 中创建 HelmHairAdjustList 字段，并将复制内容作为其值。

JSON parsing structure for Extra Layered Armor:\n'BodyArmorName' is used to specify which armor the custom armor is layering to.\n'BodyArmorMeshPath', 'BodyArmorMaterialPath', 'BodyArmorAltMaterialPath',\n'BodyArmorChainPath', 'BodyArmorAltChainPath', 'BodyArmorClspPath', 'BodyArmorGpucPath',\n'BodyArmorJcnsPath' (This has a special parsing structure.\nYou can use as many Jcns files as you want, just be sure to include a suffix for each individual entry.\n(example: 'BodyArmorJcnsPath1', 'BodyArmorJcnsPath2')\nThe value for these entries also have a special parsing structure.\nAn example being 'MyArmorMod\\JointConstraintsFile.jcns(true)((2))(((1.0)))'\nThis can be broken down into path (string), enable update timing override (boolean),\nupdate timing mode (int between 0-3), blend rate (System.Single).\nMore info for what these are set to is displayed within the ELA information.\n'BodyArmorChainCollisionTarget', 'BodyArmorAltChainCollisionTarget' (enum) 0 = None, 1 = Extern, 2 = All\n'BodyArmorGpucCollisionTarget' (enum) 0 = None, 1 = Extern, 2 = All, 3 = Max\n'BodyArmorCustomLabelsID' (string with custom ID, these can be set the same as 'ArmorName' inside parts enable\nand custom bone label json to match the identifiers and use custom labels).\nThese are also used for currently equipped display\nlabels if set to view them from ELA Settings.\nThese can all be set the same for other parts except\nreplace the Body prefix with Arm, Leg, Waist, Slinger or Helm.\nSlinger doesn't have the word 'Armor' in it for the keys.\n'HelmArmorEnableHairJointAdjustment' controls wether the hair joints are scaled/adjusted for helms.\nThis is what is being done sometimes when parts of the hair don't stick through helms.\n'HelmHairAdjustList' (AdjustListPreset), paste in your helm hair adjust preset as the value.\nIt is meant for joint parameters and hair adjustment for the helm.\n'HelmArmorDisableHair' (boolean value), fully enable/disable the hair.\n'HelmArmorDisableHairOtherParts' (boolean value), fully disable the hair or hair other parts, sometimes used with specific armors.\n'HelmArmorDisableEyebrows' (boolean value), fully enable/disable the eyebrows.\n'HelmArmorDisableEyelashes' (boolean value), fully enable/disable eyelashes.\n'HelmArmorEarObjScale' (System.Single), adjusts character ears scale.\n'HelmArmorBackHeadObjScale' (System.Single), adjusts the scale of the back of the character's head.\n'HelmArmorHeadObjScale' (System.Single), adjusts the scale of the character's head.\n'ArmArmorDisableSlinger' (boolean value), and 'WaistArmorHideLight' (boolean value).\nThe alt chains can be used to swap to a secondary chain depending on how parts enabled are set.\nThis is useful if you have something like a bra holding some tits, and you remove it with parts enabled,\nthe tits would naturally sway more, hence swapping the chain.\nYou don't have to specify in JSON for things you don't use.\nOr you can just set the value as 'nil' and that will also skip over/not use\na certain parameter like if you didn't want to set GPUC for example.\nPaths are relative to natives\\STM.\nELA data goes in reframework/data/FBSStartupData/ExtraLayeredArmor/*WhateverName*.json.\nThe Save Armor Pigmentation Colors button saves the edits\nto armor pigmentation color to an external file\nso that it can be loaded when re-launching the game.\nThe button can be clicked after making color edits\nthat use the game's built in armor pigmentation system.\nIf you are using the ELA armor system, color edits for the vanilla\narmor(s) used with ELA will also need to be saved.\n(As they now use the ELA Armor Pigmentation System).\nValues are captured when making color edits\nvia the game's regular armor pigmentation system.\nIf a slinger is shared between slot x and vanilla arm armors,\nand the pigmentation is showing up on both of them when switching,\nI don't consider that a bug. If you want to separate them,\nadd the same slinger separatly for the arm armor via ELA.\n'Bone Sliders Affect Vanilla Armor Selected?' is an option that enables/disables sliders to\naffect ONLY the vanilla armor(s) that ELA is being used with.\nKeep in mind, when you use the button(s) to manually cycle armors, this is independent of\nwhat is selected with the combo boxes.\nThe manual cycle buttons behave the same as keybinds do.\nResetting scripts in the middle of the normal gameplay can cause the skin color to be\nset to differently than your character's normal skin color.\nIf this occurs you can return to title screen and re-enter the game.\nNote, that for alt chains/materials, you do not need to specify\nthe same chains/materials multiple times for them.\nIf you don't have paths specified for them, they won't be switched\nto even when enabling the alt versions.\nSo, as an example, if you have only one chain file to use,\nand don't wish to have an alternate,\nyou can just specifiy the chain path and leave the alt chain path alone.	ELA（额外外观防具）的 JSON 结构：\nBodyArmorName 指定被叠加的原版防具。资源路径字段包括 BodyArmorMeshPath、BodyArmorMaterialPath、BodyArmorAltMaterialPath、BodyArmorChainPath、BodyArmorAltChainPath、BodyArmorClspPath、BodyArmorGpucPath。\nBodyArmorJcnsPath 支持多个文件，使用 BodyArmorJcnsPath1、BodyArmorJcnsPath2 等后缀。示例 MyArmorMod\\JointConstraintsFile.jcns(true)((2))(((1.0))) 依次表示路径、是否覆盖更新时间、更新模式（0～3）、混合比例（System.Single）。\nBodyArmorCustomLabelsID 是自定义标识，可与部件显隐及自定义骨骼标签 JSON 的 ArmorName 对应；开启相关显示时，也用于当前装备的显示名称。\n其他部位将 Body 前缀改为 Arm、Leg、Waist、Slinger 或 Helm；Slinger 的字段名不包含 Armor。\nHelmArmorEnableHairJointAdjustment 控制头盔是否调整头发关节；HelmHairAdjustList 接收头发调整预设。HelmArmorDisableHair、HelmArmorDisableEyebrows、HelmArmorDisableEyelashes 控制头发、眉毛、睫毛显隐。HelmArmorEarObjScale、HelmArmorBackHeadObjScale、HelmArmorHeadObjScale 分别控制耳朵、后脑和头部缩放。ArmArmorDisableSlinger 控制投射器，WaistArmorHideLight 控制腰灯。\n可按部件显隐状态切换备用物理链，例如移除内衣后采用不同摆动效果。不使用的字段可省略或设为字符串 'nil'。路径相对于 natives\\STM。\nELA 数据目录：reframework/data/FBSStartupData/ExtraLayeredArmor/*WhateverName*.json\n修改游戏内防具配色后，点击“保存防具配色”，写入外部文件以供下次启动加载。与 ELA 关联的原版防具配色也需要保存。如果额外槽位和原版手臂共享投射器，配色可能同时变化；需要独立配色时，请通过 ELA 为手臂单独添加投射器。\n“骨骼滑块影响所选原版防具”仅控制与 ELA 关联的原版防具。手动轮换按钮与快捷键行为一致，独立于下拉框中的选择。\n备用物理链和材质无需重复填写相同路径。未提供备用路径时，即使启用备用选项也不会切换；只有一个文件时只填主路径即可。\nBodyArmorChainCollisionTarget 与 BodyArmorAltChainCollisionTarget：0＝无，1＝外部，2＝全部。BodyArmorGpucCollisionTarget：0＝无，1＝外部，2＝全部，3＝Max。HelmArmorDisableHairOtherParts 控制部分防具的其他头发部件。\n正常游玩过程中重置脚本可能导致肤色偏离角色原色，遇到后请返回标题画面再进入游戏。

JSON parsing structure for Extra Layered Weapons:\n'WeaponName', Used to specify what weapon path (it0000_0024_0 for example) it will be layered over.\n'WeaponMeshPath, 'WeaponMaterialPath', 'WeaponChainPath'.\nIf you don't want to use one of those, it can be left empty or set to a value of 'nil' (string value).\nPaths are relative to natives\\STM.\nSame JSON parsing structure for sub parent weapons.\nFolder for ELW data: reframework/data/FBSStartupData/ExtraLayeredWeapon/*Whatever*.json\nFolder for ELW sub parent data:\nreframework/data/FBSStartupData/ExtraLayeredSubParentWeapon/*Whatever*.json=ELW（额外外观武器）的 JSON：WeaponName 指定被叠加的武器路径，例如 it0000_0024_0；WeaponMeshPath、WeaponMaterialPath、WeaponChainPath 分别指定网格、材质与物理链。未使用的字段可留空或设为字符串 'nil'。路径相对于 natives\\STM，附属武器使用相同结构。\n主武器数据目录：reframework/data/FBSStartupData/ExtraLayeredWeapon/*Whatever*.json\n附属武器数据目录：reframework/data/FBSStartupData/ExtraLayeredSubParentWeapon/*Whatever*.json
One of the main uses of adding a new fbxskel (done under the 'FBXSkels' dropdown)\nis to add compatibility with animation mods.\nSo that way, the same main fbxskel file(s) do not need to be replaced for added bones.\nThis will allow multiple different animation mods with custom fbxskels to not have conflicting fbxskels,\ngiven that they are custom pathed and loaded.\nThe custom fbxskels can be switched on autoswitch for a specific animation swap body.=在此使用自定义 FBX 骨架主要用于兼容动画模组：通过独立路径加载，避免不同模组为了增加骨骼而覆盖同一个主骨架文件。\n自定义骨架可通过自动切换功能，关联到指定的动画替换身体。

EO for Mina is based off of the torso armor, ch03_008_0012.\nThis slot if meant to be used as a whole body mesh,\nhow some of the other NPCs are handled.\nOther parts can be disabled under parts enabled.=Mina 的额外服装以防具 ch03_008_0012 为基础。该槽位按全身网格使用，与部分其他 NPC 的处理方式一致。其他部件可在部件显隐设置中关闭。
Preset can be overwritten or deleted manually via the Reframework/data/MinaBSPresets/*PresetCategory*/*YourPreset*.json folder=可在 Reframework/data/MinaBSPresets/*PresetCategory*/*YourPreset*.json 目录中手动覆盖或删除预设。
Custom Bones can be added to Aida's mesh and then adjusted here.\nThe paired custom bones (i.e. Custom Bone 1 & 2) are meant to be\nused symmetrically. The naming scheme for the bones is 'CustomBone1', etc...=可在 Aida 的网格中添加自定义骨骼，并在此调整。Custom Bone 1 & 2 等成对骨骼应对称使用，骨骼命名格式为 CustomBone1 等。
JSON can be provided inside FBSMiscNPCs\\AidaBSStartupData\\CustomBoneNames\\Body\\ to give custom bones names/labels.\nExmample labels/parsing structure:\n'OutfitName: Default Outfit',\n'CustomBone1&2Label': 'Whatever Bone Name'=可在 FBSMiscNPCs\\AidaBSStartupData\\CustomBoneNames\\Body\\ 中提供 JSON，为自定义骨骼指定名称／标签。\n标签及解析结构示例：\n'OutfitName: Default Outfit',\n'CustomBone1&2Label': 'Whatever Bone Name'
Before modifying the Aida custom bones, I encourage you to check out modifying the regular,\nother bones first, as the Aida bones are parented to them.=调整 Aida 自定义骨骼前，建议先尝试其他常规骨骼，因为这些自定义骨骼是常规骨骼的子级。
\nThese sliders are setup and meant to be used with Nude Aida mod, however if the bones/and or chains are setup for it, these sliders can be used for them.=这些滑块针对 Nude Aida 模组设计。其他资源采用兼容的骨骼或物理链结构时，也可使用这些滑块。
The Nude Aida specific custom bone names are L_FNMCustomButtBone1, R_FNMCustomButtBone1, L_FNMCustomThighBone1, L_FNMCustomThighBone1, etc.=该身体模组的专用骨骼名称为 L_FNMCustomButtBone1, R_FNMCustomButtBone1, L_FNMCustomThighBone1, L_FNMCustomThighBone1, etc.
The Nude Aida-named custom bones aren't necessarily needed for the chain groups (jiggle settings to work).=物理链组的摆动设置不一定需要这些身体模组专用名称的自定义骨骼。
Parts enabled are saved automatically when making adjustments.\nPresets can be found at reframework\\data\\AidaBSPresets\\PartsEnablePresets\\\nCustom labels can be used.\nJSON data will need to be created (with any non-conflicting given filename) and\nplaced inside reframework\\data\\FBSMiscNPCs\\AidaBSStartupData\\PartsEnableNames\\Body\\\nLabels can be specified with "MeshPartLabel0": "Base Body",\n"MeshPartLabel1": "Outer Jacket" for the mesh part example.\nMaterialPartsLabel0 being an example for the materials.=调整部件显隐时会自动保存。预设位于 reframework\\data\\AidaBSPresets\\PartsEnablePresets\\。\n可在 reframework\\data\\FBSMiscNPCs\\AidaBSStartupData\\PartsEnableNames\\Body\\ 中放置文件名不冲突的 JSON，为部件提供自定义标签。网格部件示例为 MeshPartLabel0: Base Body、MeshPartLabel1: Outer Jacket；材质部件使用 MaterialPartsLabel0 等字段。
Outfit Mesh Path: art/model/character/ch04/2/01/02/02/ch04_017_0000.mesh=服装网格路径：art/model/character/ch04/2/01/02/02/ch04_017_0000.mesh
Outfit MDF Path: art/model/character/ch04/2/01/02/02/ch04_017_0000.mdf2=服装材质路径：art/model/character/ch04/2/01/02/02/ch04_017_0000.mdf2
Outfit Chain Path: art/model/character/ch04/2/01/02/02/ch04_017_0000.chain2=服装物理链路径：art/model/character/ch04/2/01/02/02/ch04_017_0000.chain2
Outfit Jcns Paths: art/model/character/ch04/2/01/02/02/ch04_017_0000.jcns(true)((0))(((1.0)))=服装关节约束路径：art/model/character/ch04/2/01/02/02/ch04_017_0000.jcns(true)((0))(((1.0)))
EO data goes in reframework/data/FBSMiscNPCs/AidaBSStartupData/ExtraOutfits/WhateverName.json.=EO（额外服装）数据放在 reframework/data/FBSMiscNPCs/AidaBSStartupData/ExtraOutfits/WhateverName.json。
For current slider values, doesn't save to the preset file. Doesn't affect Aida Nude Mod specific settings.=仅调整当前滑块数值，不写入预设文件，也不影响 Aida 身体模组专用设置。
Preset can be overwritten or deleted manually via the Reframework/data/FBSMiscNPCs/AidaBSPresets/*PresetCategory*/*YourPreset*.json folder=可在 Reframework/data/FBSMiscNPCs/AidaBSPresets/*PresetCategory*/*YourPreset*.json 目录中手动覆盖或删除预设。
If you need to find who Aida is, try using the button\n'Make Giant ('Find Me' Test)' in the extra settings.=如果不知道 Aida 在哪里，可尝试额外设置中的“变为巨人（定位测试）”按钮。
Custom Bones can be added to Carla's mesh and then adjusted here.\nThe paired custom bones (i.e. Custom Bone 1 & 2) are meant to be\nused symmetrically. The naming scheme for the bones is 'CustomBone1', etc...=可在 Carla 的网格中添加自定义骨骼，并在此调整。Custom Bone 1 & 2 等成对骨骼应对称使用，骨骼命名格式为 CustomBone1 等。
JSON can be provided inside FBSMiscNPCs\\CarlaBSStartupData\\CustomBoneNames\\Body\\ to give custom bones names/labels.\nExmample labels/parsing structure:\n'OutfitName: Default Outfit',\n'CustomBone1&2Label': 'Whatever Bone Name'=可在 FBSMiscNPCs\\CarlaBSStartupData\\CustomBoneNames\\Body\\ 中提供 JSON，为自定义骨骼指定名称／标签。\n标签及解析结构示例：\n'OutfitName: Default Outfit',\n'CustomBone1&2Label': 'Whatever Bone Name'
Before modifying the Carla custom bones, I encourage you to check out modifying the regular,\nother bones first, as the Carla bones are parented to them.=调整 Carla 自定义骨骼前，建议先尝试其他常规骨骼，因为这些自定义骨骼是常规骨骼的子级。
\nThese sliders are setup and meant to be used with Nude Carla mod, however if the bones/and or chains are setup for it, these sliders can be used for them.=这些滑块针对 Nude Carla 模组设计。其他资源采用兼容的骨骼或物理链结构时，也可使用这些滑块。
The Nude Carla specific custom bone names are L_FNMCustomButtBone1, R_FNMCustomButtBone1, L_FNMCustomThighBone1, L_FNMCustomThighBone1, etc.=该身体模组的专用骨骼名称为 L_FNMCustomButtBone1, R_FNMCustomButtBone1, L_FNMCustomThighBone1, L_FNMCustomThighBone1, etc.
The Nude Carla-named custom bones aren't necessarily needed for the chain groups (jiggle settings to work).=物理链组的摆动设置不一定需要这些身体模组专用名称的自定义骨骼。
Parts enabled are saved automatically when making adjustments.\nPresets can be found at reframework\\data\\CarlaBSPresets\\PartsEnablePresets\\\nCustom labels can be used.\nJSON data will need to be created (with any non-conflicting given filename) and\nplaced inside reframework\\data\\FBSMiscNPCs\\CarlaBSStartupData\\PartsEnableNames\\Body\\\nLabels can be specified with "MeshPartLabel0": "Base Body",\n"MeshPartLabel1": "Outer Jacket" for the mesh part example.\nMaterialPartsLabel0 being an example for the materials.=调整部件显隐时会自动保存。预设位于 reframework\\data\\CarlaBSPresets\\PartsEnablePresets\\。\n可在 reframework\\data\\FBSMiscNPCs\\CarlaBSStartupData\\PartsEnableNames\\Body\\ 中放置文件名不冲突的 JSON，为部件提供自定义标签。网格部件示例为 MeshPartLabel0: Base Body、MeshPartLabel1: Outer Jacket；材质部件使用 MaterialPartsLabel0 等字段。

Parts Enabled are saved on a per-armor basis and values that are set will\nchange depending on what you have equipped and saved.\nYou do not need to save your body sliders preset to save these changes.\nSettings for the particular armor are saved upon clicking the checkboxes\nYour values set for parts enabled are not tied/saved to the body preset.\nThey data for them, however, be found at\nReframework\\data\\MultiplayerSlidersP1FBSPresets\\BodyPartsEnable\\PartsEnable.json\nThis is a master preset that will update dynamically with the changes you've made\nand what armor it belongs to.\nCustom labels for parts enable can be set on a per armor basis.\nThe location for the json file indicated the info to do so needs to be placed at\nreframework\\data\\MultiplayerSlidersP1FBSStartupData\\PartsEnableNames\\Body\\\n"ArmorName": "ch03_002_0002",\n"MaterialPartLabel0": "Upper Torso Armor"\nMeshPartLabel0 being an example for the mesh parts.=部件显隐按防具分别保存，会随当前装备及已保存设置切换。点击复选框即保存，无需保存身体或腿部滑块预设，且不与该预设绑定。数据记录在 Reframework\\data\\MultiplayerSlidersP1FBSPresets\\BodyPartsEnable\\PartsEnable.json，该主预设会自动更新。自定义标签的 JSON 放在 reframework\\data\\MultiplayerSlidersP1FBSStartupData\\PartsEnableNames\\Body\\，使用 ArmorName 指定防具，MaterialPartLabel0 指定材质部件名称；网格部件使用 MeshPartLabel0 等字段。
Parts Enabled that are set to invisible/unchecked and marked with\n'(False for ELA Alt Chain Usage)' use specified alternate chain files for extra\nlayered armor. This is useful if, for example, you had hard metal plate\ncovering boobs and then wanted to remove the plate. Obviously\nthere would be a difference in jiggle hence why disabling\na part specified can automatically swap chain files.=标有“False for ELA Alt Chain Usage”的部件取消勾选后，将为额外外观防具切换指定的备用物理链。例如移除胸甲或裤装后，摆动效果需要变化，可通过该功能自动切换对应的 Chain 文件。

Combine Fem Nude Body and Leg\nPresets for Nude Alma/Gemma\nSliders (Does not change current presets)\nCheck for:\nReframework/data/FemNudeMultiplayerSlidersP1FBSPresets/Combined/CombinedPreset.json\nand transfer to the Alma and/or Gemma presets folder=合并女性身体模组的身体与腿部预设，供阿尔玛／杰玛滑块使用，不会改变当前预设。\n合并结果：Reframework/data/FemNudeMultiplayerSlidersP1FBSPresets/Combined/CombinedPreset.json\n请将其复制到对应角色的预设目录。
JSON parsing structure for Extra Layered Armor:\n'BodyArmorName' is used to specify which armor the custom armor is layering to.\n'BodyArmorMeshPath', 'BodyArmorMaterialPath', 'BodyArmorAltMaterialPath',\n'BodyArmorChainPath', 'BodyArmorAltChainPath', 'BodyArmorClspPath', 'BodyArmorGpucPath',\n'BodyArmorJcnsPath' (This has a special parsing structure.\nYou can use as many Jcns files as you want, just be sure to include a suffix for each individual entry.\n(example: 'BodyArmorJcnsPath1', 'BodyArmorJcnsPath2')\nThe value for these entries also have a special parsing structure.\nAn example being 'MyArmorMod\\JointConstraintsFile.jcns(true)((2))(((1.0)))'\nThis can be broken down into path (string), enable update timing override (boolean),\nupdate timing mode (int between 0-3), blend rate (System.Single).\nMore info for what these are set to is displayed within the ELA information.\n'BodyArmorCustomLabelsID' (string with custom ID, these can be set the same as 'ArmorName' inside parts enable\nand custom bone label json to match the identifiers and use custom labels)\nThese are also used for currently equipped display\nlabels if set to view them from ELA Settings.\nThese can all be set the same for other parts except\nreplace the Body prefix with Arm, Leg, Waist, Slinger or Helm.\nSlinger doesn't have the word 'Armor' in it for the keys.\nSpecial parsing keys include 'HelmArmorEnableHairAdjustController' (controls wether the head, ears, and back of head is adjusted for the helm (boolean value))\n'HelmArmorEnableHairJointAdjustment' controls wether the hair joints are scaled/adjusted for helms.\nThis is what is being done sometimes when parts of the hair don't stick through helms.\n'HelmHairAdjustList' (AdjustListPreset), paste in your helm hair adjust preset as the value.\nIt is meant for joint parameters and hair adjustment for the helm.\n'HelmArmorDisableHair' (boolean value), fully enable/disable the hair.\n'HelmArmorDisableEyebrows' (boolean value), fully enable/disable the eyebrows.\n'HelmArmorDisableEyelashes' (boolean value), fully enable/disable eyelashes.\n'HelmArmorEarObjScale' (System.Single), adjusts character ears scale.\n'HelmArmorBackHeadObjScale' (System.Single), adjusts the scale of the back of the character's head.\n'HelmArmorHeadObjScale' (System.Single), adjusts the scale of the character's head.\n'ArmArmorDisableSlinger' (boolean value), and 'WaistArmorHideLight' (boolean value).\nThe alt chains can be used to swap to a secondary chain depending on how parts enabled are set.\nThis is useful if you have something like a bra holding some tits, and you remove it with parts enabled,\nthe tits would naturally sway more, hence swapping the chain.\nYou don't have to specify in JSON for things you don't use.\nOr you can just set the value as 'nil' and that will also skip over/not use\na certain parameter like if you didn't want to set GPUC for example.\nPaths are relative to natives\\STM.\nELA data goes in reframework/data/MultiplayerSlidersP1FBSStartupData/ExtraLayeredArmor/*WhateverName*.json.\nThe Save Armor Pigmentation Colors button saves the edits\nto armor pigmentation color to an external file\nso that it can be loaded when re-launching the game.\nThe button can be clicked after making color edits\nthat use the game's built in armor pigmentation system.\nIf you are using the ELA armor system, color edits for the vanilla\narmor(s) used with ELA will also need to be saved.\n(As they now use the ELA Armor Pigmentation System).\nValues are captured when making color edits\nvia the game's regular armor pigmentation system.\nIf a slinger is shared between slot x and vanilla arm armors,\nand the pigmentation is showing up on both of them when switching,\nI don't consider that a bug. If you want to separate them,\nadd the same slinger separatly for the arm armor via ELA.\n'Bone Sliders Affect Vanilla Armor Selected?' is an option that enables/disables sliders to\naffect ONLY the vanilla armor(s) that ELA is being used with.\nKeep in mind, when you use the button(s) to manually cycle armors, this is independent of\nwhat is selected with the combo boxes.\nThe manual cycle buttons behave the same as keybinds do.\nNote, that for alt chains/materials, you do not need to specify\nthe same chains/materials multiple times for them.\nIf you don't have paths specified for them, they won't be switched\nto even when enabling the alt versions.\nSo, as an example, if you have only one chain file to use,\nand don't wish to have an alternate,\nyou can just specifiy the chain path and leave the alt chain path alone.=ELA（额外外观防具）的 JSON 结构：\nBodyArmorName 指定被叠加的原版防具。资源路径字段包括 BodyArmorMeshPath、BodyArmorMaterialPath、BodyArmorAltMaterialPath、BodyArmorChainPath、BodyArmorAltChainPath、BodyArmorClspPath、BodyArmorGpucPath。\nBodyArmorJcnsPath 支持多个文件，使用 BodyArmorJcnsPath1、BodyArmorJcnsPath2 等后缀。示例 MyArmorMod\\JointConstraintsFile.jcns(true)((2))(((1.0))) 依次表示路径、是否覆盖更新时间、更新模式（0～3）、混合比例（System.Single）。\nBodyArmorCustomLabelsID 是自定义标识，可与部件显隐及自定义骨骼标签 JSON 的 ArmorName 对应；开启相关显示时，也用于当前装备的显示名称。\n其他部位将 Body 前缀改为 Arm、Leg、Waist、Slinger 或 Helm；Slinger 的字段名不包含 Armor。\nHelmArmorEnableHairJointAdjustment 控制头盔是否调整头发关节；HelmHairAdjustList 接收头发调整预设。HelmArmorDisableHair、HelmArmorDisableEyebrows、HelmArmorDisableEyelashes 控制头发、眉毛、睫毛显隐。HelmArmorEarObjScale、HelmArmorBackHeadObjScale、HelmArmorHeadObjScale 分别控制耳朵、后脑和头部缩放。ArmArmorDisableSlinger 控制投射器，WaistArmorHideLight 控制腰灯。\n可按部件显隐状态切换备用物理链，例如移除内衣后采用不同摆动效果。不使用的字段可省略或设为字符串 'nil'。路径相对于 natives\\STM。\nELA 数据目录：reframework/data/MultiplayerSlidersP1FBSStartupData/ExtraLayeredArmor/*WhateverName*.json\n修改游戏内防具配色后，点击“保存防具配色”，写入外部文件以供下次启动加载。与 ELA 关联的原版防具配色也需要保存。如果额外槽位和原版手臂共享投射器，配色可能同时变化；需要独立配色时，请通过 ELA 为手臂单独添加投射器。\n“骨骼滑块影响所选原版防具”仅控制与 ELA 关联的原版防具。手动轮换按钮与快捷键行为一致，独立于下拉框中的选择。\n备用物理链和材质无需重复填写相同路径。未提供备用路径时，即使启用备用选项也不会切换；只有一个文件时只填主路径即可。\nHelmArmorEnableHairAdjustController 控制是否按头盔调整头部、耳朵与后脑。
JSON parsing structure for Extra Layered Weapons:\n'WeaponName', Used to specify what weapon path (it0000_0024_0 for example) it will be layered over.\n'WeaponMeshPath, 'WeaponMaterialPath', 'WeaponChainPath'.\nIf you don't want to use one of those, it can be left empty or set to a value of 'nil' (string value).\nPaths are relative to natives\\STM.\nSame JSON parsing structure for sub parent weapons.\nFolder for ELW data: reframework/data/MultiplayerSlidersP1FBSStartupData/ExtraLayeredWeapon/*Whatever*.json\nFolder for ELW sub parent data:\nreframework/data/MultiplayerSlidersP1FBSStartupData/ExtraLayeredSubParentWeapon/*Whatever*.json=ELW（额外外观武器）的 JSON：WeaponName 指定被叠加的武器路径，例如 it0000_0024_0；WeaponMeshPath、WeaponMaterialPath、WeaponChainPath 分别指定网格、材质与物理链。未使用的字段可留空或设为字符串 'nil'。路径相对于 natives\\STM，附属武器使用相同结构。\n主武器数据目录：reframework/data/MultiplayerSlidersP1FBSStartupData/ExtraLayeredWeapon/*Whatever*.json\n附属武器数据目录：reframework/data/MultiplayerSlidersP1FBSStartupData/ExtraLayeredSubParentWeapon/*Whatever*.json
Note that this does not apply to multiple occurances of buff glow effects such as using demon powder and hardshell powder together.\n(This is for effeciency reasons since settings for multiple glow effects would have to be updated each frame).=此项不作用于同时存在的多种增益发光，例如同时使用鬼人粉尘与硬化粉尘。这是出于性能考虑，因为处理多个发光特效需要每帧更新设置。
Combine Body, Leg, and Face\nPresets for use with Alma/Gemma/Olivia Sliders\n(Does not change current presets)\nCheck for:\nReframework/data/MultiplayerSlidersP1FBSPresets/Combined/CombinedPreset.json\nand transfer to the Alma and/or Gemma presets folder=合并身体、腿部与面部预设，供阿尔玛／杰玛／奥利维亚滑块使用，不会改变当前预设。\n合并结果：Reframework/data/MultiplayerSlidersP1FBSPresets/Combined/CombinedPreset.json\n请将其复制到对应角色的预设目录。

This is most likely due to the rest of FBS's Reframework/data folder missing.\nMake sure the whole data folder from the mod in the correct location and as an example, WeaponVisibility.json can be found at the aformentioned path.=身形滑块模组数据不完整！\n未找到 reframework/data/MultiplayerSlidersP1FBSPresets/Weapon/WeaponVisibility.json。这通常表示模组的 reframework/data 目录缺失或位置错误。请确认完整数据目录已放在正确位置，并核对该路径中的 WeaponVisibility.json。
JSON can be provided inside MultiplayerSlidersP2FBSStartupData\\CustomBoneNames\\Body\\ to give custom bones names/labels.\nExmample labels/parsing structure:\n'ArmorName': 'ch03_002_0012',\n'CustomBone1&2Label': 'Whatever Bone Name'=可在 MultiplayerSlidersP2FBSStartupData\\CustomBoneNames\\Body\\ 中提供 JSON，为自定义骨骼指定名称／标签。\n标签及解析结构示例：\n'ArmorName': 'ch03_002_0012',\n'CustomBone1&2Label': 'Whatever Bone Name'
Parts Enabled are saved on a per-armor basis and values that are set will\nchange depending on what you have equipped and saved.\nYou do not need to save your body sliders preset to save these changes.\nSettings for the particular armor are saved upon clicking the checkboxes\nYour values set for parts enabled are not tied/saved to the body preset.\nThey data for them, however, be found at\nReframework\\data\\MultiplayerSlidersP2FBSPresets\\BodyPartsEnable\\PartsEnable.json\nThis is a master preset that will update dynamically with the changes you've made\nand what armor it belongs to.\nCustom labels for parts enable can be set on a per armor basis.\nThe location for the json file indicated the info to do so needs to be placed at\nreframework\\data\\MultiplayerSlidersP2FBSStartupData\\PartsEnableNames\\Body\\\n"ArmorName": "ch03_002_0002",\n"MaterialPartLabel0": "Upper Torso Armor"\nMeshPartLabel0 being an example for the mesh parts.=部件显隐按防具分别保存，会随当前装备及已保存设置切换。点击复选框即保存，无需保存身体或腿部滑块预设，且不与该预设绑定。数据记录在 Reframework\\data\\MultiplayerSlidersP2FBSPresets\\BodyPartsEnable\\PartsEnable.json，该主预设会自动更新。自定义标签的 JSON 放在 reframework\\data\\MultiplayerSlidersP2FBSStartupData\\PartsEnableNames\\Body\\，使用 ArmorName 指定防具，MaterialPartLabel0 指定材质部件名称；网格部件使用 MeshPartLabel0 等字段。
Parts Enabled that are set to invisible/unchecked and marked with\n'(False for ELA Alt Chain Usage)' use specified alternate chain files for extra\nlayered armor. This is useful if, for example, you had hard metal plate covering\nboobs and then wanted to remove the plate. Obviously there would\nbe a difference in jiggle hence why disabling a part specified can automatically\nswap chain files.=标有“False for ELA Alt Chain Usage”的部件取消勾选后，将为额外外观防具切换指定的备用物理链。例如移除胸甲或裤装后，摆动效果需要变化，可通过该功能自动切换对应的 Chain 文件。

Combine Fem Nude Body and Leg\nPresets for Nude Alma/Gemma\nSliders (Does not change current presets)\nCheck for:\nReframework/data/FemNudeMultiplayerSlidersP2FBSPresets/Combined/CombinedPreset.json\nand transfer to the Alma and/or Gemma presets folder=合并女性身体模组的身体与腿部预设，供阿尔玛／杰玛滑块使用，不会改变当前预设。\n合并结果：Reframework/data/FemNudeMultiplayerSlidersP2FBSPresets/Combined/CombinedPreset.json\n请将其复制到对应角色的预设目录。
JSON parsing structure for Extra Layered Armor:\n'BodyArmorName' is used to specify which armor the custom armor is layering to.\n'BodyArmorMeshPath', 'BodyArmorMaterialPath', 'BodyArmorAltMaterialPath',\n'BodyArmorChainPath', 'BodyArmorAltChainPath', 'BodyArmorClspPath', 'BodyArmorGpucPath',\n'BodyArmorJcnsPath' (This has a special parsing structure.\nYou can use as many Jcns files as you want, just be sure to include a suffix for each individual entry.\n(example: 'BodyArmorJcnsPath1', 'BodyArmorJcnsPath2')\nThe value for these entries also have a special parsing structure.\nAn example being 'MyArmorMod\\JointConstraintsFile.jcns(true)((2))(((1.0)))'\nThis can be broken down into path (string), enable update timing override (boolean),\nupdate timing mode (int between 0-3), blend rate (System.Single).\nMore info for what these are set to is displayed within the ELA information.\n'BodyArmorCustomLabelsID' (string with custom ID, these can be set the same as 'ArmorName' inside parts enable\nand custom bone label json to match the identifiers and use custom labels)\nThese are also used for currently equipped display\nlabels if set to view them from ELA Settings.\nThese can all be set the same for other parts except\nreplace the Body prefix with Arm, Leg, Waist, Slinger or Helm.\nSlinger doesn't have the word 'Armor' in it for the keys.\nSpecial parsing keys include 'HelmArmorEnableHairAdjustController' (controls wether the head, ears, and back of head is adjusted for the helm (boolean value))\n'HelmArmorEnableHairJointAdjustment' controls wether the hair joints are scaled/adjusted for helms.\nThis is what is being done sometimes when parts of the hair don't stick through helms.\n'HelmHairAdjustList' (AdjustListPreset), paste in your helm hair adjust preset as the value.\nIt is meant for joint parameters and hair adjustment for the helm.\n'HelmArmorDisableHair' (boolean value), fully enable/disable the hair.\n'HelmArmorDisableEyebrows' (boolean value), fully enable/disable the eyebrows.\n'HelmArmorDisableEyelashes' (boolean value), fully enable/disable eyelashes.\n'HelmArmorEarObjScale' (System.Single), adjusts character ears scale.\n'HelmArmorBackHeadObjScale' (System.Single), adjusts the scale of the back of the character's head.\n'HelmArmorHeadObjScale' (System.Single), adjusts the scale of the character's head.\n'ArmArmorDisableSlinger' (boolean value), and 'WaistArmorHideLight' (boolean value).\nThe alt chains can be used to swap to a secondary chain depending on how parts enabled are set.\nThis is useful if you have something like a bra holding some tits, and you remove it with parts enabled,\nthe tits would naturally sway more, hence swapping the chain.\nYou don't have to specify in JSON for things you don't use.\nOr you can just set the value as 'nil' and that will also skip over/not use\na certain parameter like if you didn't want to set GPUC for example.\nPaths are relative to natives\\STM.\nELA data goes in reframework/data/MultiplayerSlidersP2FBSStartupData/ExtraLayeredArmor/*WhateverName*.json.\nThe Save Armor Pigmentation Colors button saves the edits\nto armor pigmentation color to an external file\nso that it can be loaded when re-launching the game.\nThe button can be clicked after making color edits\nthat use the game's built in armor pigmentation system.\nIf you are using the ELA armor system, color edits for the vanilla\narmor(s) used with ELA will also need to be saved.\n(As they now use the ELA Armor Pigmentation System).\nValues are captured when making color edits\nvia the game's regular armor pigmentation system.\nIf a slinger is shared between slot x and vanilla arm armors,\nand the pigmentation is showing up on both of them when switching,\nI don't consider that a bug. If you want to separate them,\nadd the same slinger separatly for the arm armor via ELA.\n'Bone Sliders Affect Vanilla Armor Selected?' is an option that enables/disables sliders to\naffect ONLY the vanilla armor(s) that ELA is being used with.\nKeep in mind, when you use the button(s) to manually cycle armors, this is independent of\nwhat is selected with the combo boxes.\nThe manual cycle buttons behave the same as keybinds do.\nNote, that for alt chains/materials, you do not need to specify\nthe same chains/materials multiple times for them.\nIf you don't have paths specified for them, they won't be switched\nto even when enabling the alt versions.\nSo, as an example, if you have only one chain file to use,\nand don't wish to have an alternate,\nyou can just specifiy the chain path and leave the alt chain path alone.=ELA（额外外观防具）的 JSON 结构：\nBodyArmorName 指定被叠加的原版防具。资源路径字段包括 BodyArmorMeshPath、BodyArmorMaterialPath、BodyArmorAltMaterialPath、BodyArmorChainPath、BodyArmorAltChainPath、BodyArmorClspPath、BodyArmorGpucPath。\nBodyArmorJcnsPath 支持多个文件，使用 BodyArmorJcnsPath1、BodyArmorJcnsPath2 等后缀。示例 MyArmorMod\\JointConstraintsFile.jcns(true)((2))(((1.0))) 依次表示路径、是否覆盖更新时间、更新模式（0～3）、混合比例（System.Single）。\nBodyArmorCustomLabelsID 是自定义标识，可与部件显隐及自定义骨骼标签 JSON 的 ArmorName 对应；开启相关显示时，也用于当前装备的显示名称。\n其他部位将 Body 前缀改为 Arm、Leg、Waist、Slinger 或 Helm；Slinger 的字段名不包含 Armor。\nHelmArmorEnableHairJointAdjustment 控制头盔是否调整头发关节；HelmHairAdjustList 接收头发调整预设。HelmArmorDisableHair、HelmArmorDisableEyebrows、HelmArmorDisableEyelashes 控制头发、眉毛、睫毛显隐。HelmArmorEarObjScale、HelmArmorBackHeadObjScale、HelmArmorHeadObjScale 分别控制耳朵、后脑和头部缩放。ArmArmorDisableSlinger 控制投射器，WaistArmorHideLight 控制腰灯。\n可按部件显隐状态切换备用物理链，例如移除内衣后采用不同摆动效果。不使用的字段可省略或设为字符串 'nil'。路径相对于 natives\\STM。\nELA 数据目录：reframework/data/MultiplayerSlidersP2FBSStartupData/ExtraLayeredArmor/*WhateverName*.json\n修改游戏内防具配色后，点击“保存防具配色”，写入外部文件以供下次启动加载。与 ELA 关联的原版防具配色也需要保存。如果额外槽位和原版手臂共享投射器，配色可能同时变化；需要独立配色时，请通过 ELA 为手臂单独添加投射器。\n“骨骼滑块影响所选原版防具”仅控制与 ELA 关联的原版防具。手动轮换按钮与快捷键行为一致，独立于下拉框中的选择。\n备用物理链和材质无需重复填写相同路径。未提供备用路径时，即使启用备用选项也不会切换；只有一个文件时只填主路径即可。\nHelmArmorEnableHairAdjustController 控制是否按头盔调整头部、耳朵与后脑。
JSON parsing structure for Extra Layered Weapons:\n'WeaponName', Used to specify what weapon path (it0000_0024_0 for example) it will be layered over.\n'WeaponMeshPath, 'WeaponMaterialPath', 'WeaponChainPath'.\nIf you don't want to use one of those, it can be left empty or set to a value of 'nil' (string value).\nPaths are relative to natives\\STM.\nSame JSON parsing structure for sub parent weapons.\nFolder for ELW data: reframework/data/MultiplayerSlidersP2FBSStartupData/ExtraLayeredWeapon/*Whatever*.json\nFolder for ELW sub parent data:\nreframework/data/MultiplayerSlidersP2FBSStartupData/ExtraLayeredSubParentWeapon/*Whatever*.json=ELW（额外外观武器）的 JSON：WeaponName 指定被叠加的武器路径，例如 it0000_0024_0；WeaponMeshPath、WeaponMaterialPath、WeaponChainPath 分别指定网格、材质与物理链。未使用的字段可留空或设为字符串 'nil'。路径相对于 natives\\STM，附属武器使用相同结构。\n主武器数据目录：reframework/data/MultiplayerSlidersP2FBSStartupData/ExtraLayeredWeapon/*Whatever*.json\n附属武器数据目录：reframework/data/MultiplayerSlidersP2FBSStartupData/ExtraLayeredSubParentWeapon/*Whatever*.json

'BodyArmorAltMaterialPath',\n'BodyArmorChainPath', 'BodyArmorAltChainPath', 'BodyArmorClspPath', 'BodyArmorGpucPath',\n'BodyArmorJcnsPath' (This has a special parsing structure.\nYou can use as many Jcns files as you want, just be sure to include a suffix for each individual entry.\n(example: 'BodyArmorJcnsPath1', 'BodyArmorJcnsPath2')\nThe value for these entries also have a special parsing structure.\nAn example being 'MyArmorMod\\JointConstraintsFile.jcns(true)((2))(((1.0)))'\nThis can be broken down into path (string), enable update timing override (boolean),\nupdate timing mode (int between 0-3), blend rate (System.Single).\nMore info for what these are set to is displayed within the ELA information.\n'BodyArmorCustomLabelsID' (string with custom ID, these can be set the same as 'ArmorName' inside parts enable\nand custom bone label json to match the identifiers and use custom labels)\nThese are also used for currently equipped display\nlabels if set to view them from ELA Settings.\nThese can all be set the same for other parts except\nreplace the Body prefix with Arm, Leg, Waist, Slinger or Helm.\nSlinger doesn't have the word 'Armor' in it for the keys.\nSpecial parsing keys include 'HelmArmorEnableHairAdjustController' (controls wether the head, ears, and back of head is adjusted for the helm (boolean value))\n'HelmArmorEnableHairJointAdjustment' controls wether the hair joints are scaled/adjusted for helms.\nThis is what is being done sometimes when parts of the hair don't stick through helms.\n'HelmHairAdjustList' (AdjustListPreset), paste in your helm hair adjust preset as the value.\nIt is meant for joint parameters and hair adjustment for the helm.\n'HelmArmorDisableHair' (boolean value), fully enable/disable the hair.\n'HelmArmorDisableEyebrows' (boolean value), fully enable/disable the eyebrows.\n'HelmArmorDisableEyelashes' (boolean value), fully enable/disable eyelashes.\n'HelmArmorEarObjScale' (System.Single), adjusts character ears scale.\n'HelmArmorBackHeadObjScale' (System.Single), adjusts the scale of the back of the character's head.\n'HelmArmorHeadObjScale' (System.Single), adjusts the scale of the character's head.\n'ArmArmorDisableSlinger' (boolean value), and 'WaistArmorHideLight' (boolean value).\nThe alt chains can be used to swap to a secondary chain depending on how parts enabled are set.\nThis is useful if you have something like a bra holding some tits, and you remove it with parts enabled,\nthe tits would naturally sway more, hence swapping the chain.\nYou don't have to specify in JSON for things you don't use.\nOr you can just set the value as 'nil' and that will also skip over/not use\na certain parameter like if you didn't want to set GPUC for example.\nPaths are relative to natives\\STM.\nELA data goes in reframework/data/MultiplayerSlidersP3FBSStartupData/ExtraLayeredArmor/*WhateverName*.json.\nThe Save Armor Pigmentation Colors button saves the edits\nto armor pigmentation color to an external file\nso that it can be loaded when re-launching the game.\nThe button can be clicked after making color edits\nthat use the game's built in armor pigmentation system.\nIf you are using the ELA armor system, color edits for the vanilla\narmor(s) used with ELA will also need to be saved.\n(As they now use the ELA Armor Pigmentation System).\nValues are captured when making color edits\nvia the game's regular armor pigmentation system.\nIf a slinger is shared between slot x and vanilla arm armors,\nand the pigmentation is showing up on both of them when switching,\nI don't consider that a bug. If you want to separate them,\nadd the same slinger separatly for the arm armor via ELA.\n'Bone Sliders Affect Vanilla Armor Selected?' is an option that enables/disables sliders to\naffect ONLY the vanilla armor(s) that ELA is being used with.\nKeep in mind, when you use the button(s) to manually cycle armors, this is independent of\nwhat is selected with the combo boxes.\nThe manual cycle buttons behave the same as keybinds do.\nNote, that for alt chains/materials, you do not need to specify\nthe same chains/materials multiple times for them.\nIf you don't have paths specified for them, they won't be switched\nto even when enabling the alt versions.\nSo, as an example, if you have only one chain file to use,\nand don't wish to have an alternate,\nyou can just specifiy the chain path and leave the alt chain path alone.=ELA（额外外观防具）的 JSON 结构：\nBodyArmorName 指定被叠加的原版防具。资源路径字段包括 BodyArmorMeshPath、BodyArmorMaterialPath、BodyArmorAltMaterialPath、BodyArmorChainPath、BodyArmorAltChainPath、BodyArmorClspPath、BodyArmorGpucPath。\nBodyArmorJcnsPath 支持多个文件，使用 BodyArmorJcnsPath1、BodyArmorJcnsPath2 等后缀。示例 MyArmorMod\\JointConstraintsFile.jcns(true)((2))(((1.0))) 依次表示路径、是否覆盖更新时间、更新模式（0～3）、混合比例（System.Single）。\nBodyArmorCustomLabelsID 是自定义标识，可与部件显隐及自定义骨骼标签 JSON 的 ArmorName 对应；开启相关显示时，也用于当前装备的显示名称。\n其他部位将 Body 前缀改为 Arm、Leg、Waist、Slinger 或 Helm；Slinger 的字段名不包含 Armor。\nHelmArmorEnableHairJointAdjustment 控制头盔是否调整头发关节；HelmHairAdjustList 接收头发调整预设。HelmArmorDisableHair、HelmArmorDisableEyebrows、HelmArmorDisableEyelashes 控制头发、眉毛、睫毛显隐。HelmArmorEarObjScale、HelmArmorBackHeadObjScale、HelmArmorHeadObjScale 分别控制耳朵、后脑和头部缩放。ArmArmorDisableSlinger 控制投射器，WaistArmorHideLight 控制腰灯。\n可按部件显隐状态切换备用物理链，例如移除内衣后采用不同摆动效果。不使用的字段可省略或设为字符串 'nil'。路径相对于 natives\\STM。\nELA 数据目录：reframework/data/MultiplayerSlidersP3FBSStartupData/ExtraLayeredArmor/*WhateverName*.json\n修改游戏内防具配色后，点击“保存防具配色”，写入外部文件以供下次启动加载。与 ELA 关联的原版防具配色也需要保存。如果额外槽位和原版手臂共享投射器，配色可能同时变化；需要独立配色时，请通过 ELA 为手臂单独添加投射器。\n“骨骼滑块影响所选原版防具”仅控制与 ELA 关联的原版防具。手动轮换按钮与快捷键行为一致，独立于下拉框中的选择。\n备用物理链和材质无需重复填写相同路径。未提供备用路径时，即使启用备用选项也不会切换；只有一个文件时只填主路径即可。\nHelmArmorEnableHairAdjustController 控制是否按头盔调整头部、耳朵与后脑。
JSON parsing structure for Extra Layered Weapons:\n'WeaponName', Used to specify what weapon path (it0000_0024_0 for example) it will be layered over.\n'WeaponMeshPath, 'WeaponMaterialPath', 'WeaponChainPath'.\nIf you don't want to use one of those, it can be left empty or set to a value of 'nil' (string value).\nPaths are relative to natives\\STM.\nSame JSON parsing structure for sub parent weapons.\nFolder for ELW data: reframework/data/MultiplayerSlidersP3FBSStartupData/ExtraLayeredWeapon/*Whatever*.json\nFolder for ELW sub parent data:\nreframework/data/MultiplayerSlidersP3FBSStartupData/ExtraLayeredSubParentWeapon/*Whatever*.json=ELW（额外外观武器）的 JSON：WeaponName 指定被叠加的武器路径，例如 it0000_0024_0；WeaponMeshPath、WeaponMaterialPath、WeaponChainPath 分别指定网格、材质与物理链。未使用的字段可留空或设为字符串 'nil'。路径相对于 natives\\STM，附属武器使用相同结构。\n主武器数据目录：reframework/data/MultiplayerSlidersP3FBSStartupData/ExtraLayeredWeapon/*Whatever*.json\n附属武器数据目录：reframework/data/MultiplayerSlidersP3FBSStartupData/ExtraLayeredSubParentWeapon/*Whatever*.json

"Charge time %" sets the required time to get the max charge in percentage to the original charge time.\n The smaller the value, the faster the charging.="充能时间%"表示最大充能所需时间占原始充能时间的百分比。数值越小，充能越快。

'Safe' weapon selection limits the 3rd and 4th slots to prevent crashes introduced in TU3.=“安全”武器选择会限制第三、第四个栏位，以避免TU3更新引入的崩溃。

Disables culling of entire objects whose screen-space projected\nsize falls below the threshold set by SmallObjectCullingRatio.\n\nMore aggressive than per-triangle culling — entire draw calls\nare skipped when enabled. Disabling it forces all small distant\nobjects to render, reducing pop-in at a GPU geometry cost.=禁用小物体整体剔除：物体在屏幕上的投影尺寸\n低于SmallObjectCullingRatio阈值时原本会被剔除。\n\n这种方式比逐三角形剔除更激进，会跳过整个绘制调用。\n禁用后会强制渲染远处的小物体，减少突然出现的现象，\n代价是增加GPU几何处理负担。
Disables LOD (Level of Detail) switching in the renderer.\nAll objects render at their highest available mesh detail\nregardless of camera distance.\n\nMay improve geometry quality up close but impact is not fully\ntested — could increase GPU load in dense areas.=禁用渲染器的LOD（细节层次）切换。\n无论镜头距离远近，所有物体都会以可用的最高\n网格精度渲染。\n\n可能改善近处的模型质量，但影响尚未完整测试，\n物体密集区域的GPU负载可能增加。
