How many frames after a perfect dodge that the player is in super slow motion=完美闪避后，玩家处于超慢动作的帧数
How many frames after a step dodge before you can chain it into a roll=垫步闪避后，衔接翻滚前需等待的帧数
How many frames after a step dodge before you can start sprinting=垫步闪避后，开始冲刺前需等待的帧数
How many seconds after a perfect dive roll that slow motion will be active or counterattacks available\nGround roll and step dodge will have 85%% of this duration=完美扑身翻滚后，慢动作或反击机会的持续秒数\n地面翻滚和垫步闪避的持续时间为其 85%%
How many to add with one press of Add (default 3).=每次点击添加时增加的数量（默认3）。
How much an enemy hit shortens a running boost or drains the charge. At 0.00x hits do neither (default 1.00x).=敌人命中时缩短强化持续时间或扣除蓄积的程度。设为0.00x时，两者均不受受击影响（默认1.00x）。
How much faster interactions play. 1.0x is the game's speed (default 3.0x).=交互播放速度的倍率。1.0x为游戏原版速度（默认3.0x）。
How much of the charge starting the boost takes. At 0.00x the charge is kept (default 1.00x).=触发强化时消耗的蓄积量。设为0.00x时保留蓄积（默认1.00x）。
How much of the gauge an enemy hit or a Rikido break takes. At 0.00x hits take nothing (default 1.00x).=敌人命中或力道崩解造成的计量槽消耗。设为0.00x时，受击不消耗计量槽（默认1.00x）。
How much of the gauge an Oni skill spends. At 0.00x skills are free and fire on an empty gauge (default 1.00x).=鬼技能消耗计量槽的比例。设为0.00x时，技能不消耗计量槽，并可在空槽时发动（默认1.00x）。
How much of this mod's pawn affinity grants count: Affinity Events, the Love option,\nhigh fives above the game's amount and support pawn affinity. The game's own gains are unchanged.\n100% is the speed before 1.2.12.=此 Mod 的随从好感度增益生效比例：好感度事件、爱慕选项、\n超出游戏原有数值的击掌增益及支援随从好感度。游戏原有增益保持不变。\n100% 即 1.2.12 之前的增长速度。
How much stamina (out of 100) is exhausted by a dive roll, ground roll or step dodge=扑身翻滚、地面翻滚或垫步闪避消耗的耐力（满值为 100）
How much stamina (out of 100) is exhausted by a Free kick (Kick Open Doors mod 'Kick Anytime')=自由踢击消耗的耐力（满值为 100，使用 Kick Open Doors Mod 的“随时踢击”功能）

How much stamina is consumed by doing a dodge=一次闪避消耗的耐力
How much stamina is regained on a successful Perfect Dodge=成功完美闪避后恢复的耐力
How much the gauge fills from blue souls and other refills. A gain never rounds down to nothing (default 1.00x).=蓝魂和其他回复来源填充计量槽的数量。增加量不会因向下取整而变成零（默认1.00x）。
How much to add (negative subtracts, up to 1000 either way).\nDrag left/right to change it, or double-click to type a value.=要增加的好感度数值；负数表示扣除，增减上限均为 1000。\n左右拖动可调整数值，也可双击后直接输入。
How much your main pawn's affinity goes up when you choose this option (default 150).\nGiven when the moment ends; the option is once per in-game day.\nScaled by Pawn Affinity Gain Rate (top of the settings).=选择此选项时，主随从增加的好感度（默认 150）。\n互动结束时发放，每个游戏日可使用一次。\n受设置顶部“随从好感度增长速率”影响。
How often the collectible scan runs. Lower is quicker to show new areas, higher is cheaper (default 1.00 s).=收集品扫描的执行间隔。数值越低，新区域显示越快；数值越高，开销越低（默认1.00 s）。
How slowly / smoothly the camera tilts to see the target. Gets stronger as it approaches 1.0=镜头倾斜以看清目标时的迟缓／平滑程度，越接近 1.0 越明显
How soon the camera can switch after a previous switch (if you are still onscreen)\nCtrl+click to type-in=上次切换镜头后，允许再次切换的最短间隔（角色仍在画面内时）\n按住Ctrl并点击可输入数值
How the bullet's uptime is measured.=子弹存续的判定方式。
How the daily amount grows the longer it goes on:\nLinear: 1x, 2x, 3x... Logarithmic: grows quickly, then levels off.\nExponential: multiplied by the rate below each day.=每日数值随持续时间增长的方式：\n线性：1 倍、2 倍、3 倍……对数：先快速增长，再趋于平缓。\n指数：每天乘以下方设置的倍率。

Off leaves every cancel window and command mask at the game's own values from the next frame; nothing to undo (default on).=关闭后，从下一帧起，所有取消窗口和指令掩码均使用游戏原版数值，无需额外还原（默认开启）。
Off leaves every pickup for you to take by hand. Nothing in the game is changed either way (default on).=关闭后，需要手动拾取所有物品。无论开关如何，都不会修改游戏中的物体数据（默认开启）。
Off leaves shop rows as the game builds them, so stock and prices are back to normal from the next shop visit. Restock still works when pressed (default on).=关闭后，商店商品按原版生成，下次进入商店时恢复正常库存和价格。点击补货按钮仍然有效（默认开启）。
Off puts every animation, door, chest and elevator back to the game's own speed.=关闭后，所有交互动画、门、宝箱和升降梯均恢复游戏原版速度。
Off: only passive decay over time is blocked. Drops you cause (pickpocketing,\nattacking or grabbing NPCs, quest outcomes...) still count.\nOn: every decrease is reverted, whatever caused it (the behavior before 1.2.0).\nYour main pawn is always fully protected.=关闭：仅阻止随时间产生的被动衰减。扒窃、攻击或抓取 NPC、\n任务结果等由你引起的下降仍然生效。\n开启：无论原因如何，都会恢复所有下降的好感度，与 1.2.0 之前的行为一致。\n主随从始终受到完整保护。
Off: the strong shot charges at the game's own speed again; a charge in progress gets its original time back (default on).=关闭后，强射蓄力恢复游戏原版速度；正在进行的蓄力也会恢复原始时长（默认开启）。

When checked, affinity text is centered inside the bar.\nWhen unchecked, text appears above the bar.=勾选后，好感度文字居中显示在进度条内。\n取消勾选后，文字显示在进度条上方。

When holding 'Switch Weapon Skill'=按住“切换武器技能”时

When it ends, you and your main pawn get the same warmth a Heatstone gives,\nfor as long as a Heatstone lasts. No Heatstone is used.=结束后，你和主随从会获得与暖石相同的取暖效果，\n持续时间也与暖石相同。不会消耗暖石。
When near someone: the bar of whoever you're facing / hailing.\nBriefly: when their 'Hail' prompt appears, for a few seconds, then it fades.\nOnly on 'Hail': for a few seconds after you hail someone.\nOnly while talking: during conversations.\nAlways, on everyone nearby: a bar over every NPC (and your main pawn) in range.=靠近某人时：显示你面向或招呼的对象的好感度条。\n短暂显示：出现“招呼”提示后显示数秒，再淡出。\n仅在招呼后：招呼某人后显示数秒。\n仅交谈时：交谈期间显示。\n始终显示附近所有人：显示范围内所有 NPC（及主随从）的好感度条。
When not holding 'Switch Weapon Skill'=未按住“切换武器技能”时
When ON the game increases the contribution of temporal RT history\n(stronger accumulation), reducing noise faster and stabilising\nstatic views — but can cause ghosting/trailing on fast motion\nor rapid camera pans.\n\nDisabling it reduces smearing around moving silhouettes and\nprops in dim scenes at the cost of more noise.=启用光追历史强累积时，游戏会提高历史帧的贡献，\n从而更快降低噪点、稳定静止画面，\n但快速运动或快速转动镜头时\n可能产生重影或拖尾。\n\n禁用强累积可减少昏暗场景中移动轮廓及物体周围的拖影，\n代价是噪点增多。
When on, decay prevention protects only your main pawn and the NPCs you 'Lock'\nin the Affinity List window. Everyone else decays normally.=开启后，防衰减功能仅保护主随从，以及你在好感度列表窗口中\n“锁定”的 NPC。其他角色的好感度仍会正常衰减。
When reveal or the mod is turned off, clears the discovery flags this session set. Objects you then found yourself go dark too and return on the next walk-by (default on).=关闭收集品显示或停用模组时，清除此会话设置的发现标记。期间你亲自发现的物体也会暂时隐藏，再次经过时恢复（默认开启）。

Your character absorbs souls on their own while any are out. Off: you hold the absorb button yourself, as normal (default on).=附近有魂时，角色会自行吸收。关闭后，需要像原版一样手动按住吸收键（默认开启）。
